## Document 021: 115年9月海關進出口貿易初步統計 (2026-10-08)

Extraction status: ok

115 年 9 月海關進出口貿易初步統計 
一、進出口貿易變動 
（一）出口 
9 月出口 872.2 億美元，創歷年單月新高，較上月增 5.9%，較上年
同月增 60.9%(按新臺幣計算增 68.2%)；第3 季出口 2,449.2 億美元，
較上年同季增 44.6%；累計 1 至 9 月 6,615.5 億美元，較上年同期增
46.2%(按新臺幣計算增 49.1%)。 
（二）進口 
9 月進口 635.9 億美元，亦創歷年單月新高，較上月增 5.8%，較上
年同月增 51.7%(按新臺幣計算增 58.6%)；第 3 季進口 1,818.0 億美
元，較上年同季增 44.3%；累計 1 至 9 月 5,010.0 億美元，較上年同
期增 41.3%(按新臺幣計算增 43.8%)。 
（三）出入超 
9 月出超 236.3 億美元，為單月新高，較上年同月增 113.4 億美元；
累計 1 至 9 月出超 1,605.5 億美元，較上年同期增 625.4 億美元。

二、出口主要貨品 
（一）與上年同月比較 
由於創新科技應用商機持續擴展，推升相關供應鏈出貨動能，加上
消費性電子新品備貨需求與產品價格上漲效應之挹注， 9 月出口較
上年同月增 330.0 億美元(+60.9%)，主要變動如下： 
1. 資通與視聽產品出口 391.3 億美元(歷年單月次高)， 較上年同月增
198.6 億美元(+1.0 倍)， 其中電腦及其附屬單元增157.4 億美元(+1.1
倍)、儲存媒體增 17.6 億美元(+2.2 倍)、交換器及路由器增 10.5
億美元(+1.1 倍)。 
2. 電子零組件出口 311.3 億美元(歷年單月次高)， 較上年同月增97.1
億美元(+45.3%)，其中積體電路增 93.9 億美元(+46.1%)、印刷電
路增 1.4 億美元(+33.7%)、 電容器及電阻器增0.8 億美元(+32.0%)。 
3. 礦產品出口 22.6 億美元，較上年同月增 10.5 億美元(+86.4%)，其
中石油煉製品增 10.1 億美元(+99.0%)。 
4. 機械出口 27.4 億美元(歷年單月次高)，較上年同月增 6.4 億美元
(+30.7%)，其中生產半導體等機械增 2.7 億美元(+61.0%)、未列名
具特殊功能機械增 0.6 億美元(+73.6%)。 
5. 基本金屬及其製品出口 26.9 億美元，較上年同月增 5.0 億美元
(+22.9%)，其中銅及其製品增 2.5 億美元(+65.1%)、鋼鐵及其製品
增 1.4 億美元(+11.7%)。

（二）與上年同期比較 
1. 第 3 季出口較上年同季增 755.9 億美元(+44.6%)，以資通與視聽產
品增 367.6 億美元(+55.0%)、電子零組件增 308.4 億美元(+51.2%)
增加較多。 
2. 累計 1 至 9 月出口較上年同期增 2,089.8 億美元(+46.2%)，其中資
通與視聽產品增 1,222.2 億美元(+72.4%)、電子零組件增 726.6 億
美元(+45.7%)。

等出現結構性轉變，爰另予併計。

三、進口主要貨品 
（一）與上年同月比較 
隨 AI 產業鏈全球分工與出口引申需求擴增，電子零組件、資通產品
及半導體設備進口維持強勁，加上國際農工原料行情處於高檔，9
月進口較上年同月增 216.7 億美元(+51.7%)，主要變動如下： 
1. 農工原料進口440.5 億美元， 較上年同月增163.9億美元(+59.3%)，
其中電子零組件增 112.6 億美元(+82.5%)、電腦之零附件增 19.4
億美元(+1.5 倍)、礦產品增 17.1 億美元(+38.9%)。 
2. 資本設備進口 128.0 億美元， 較上年同月增 36.0 億美元(+39.1%)，
其中資通設備增 18.4 億美元(+75.0%)、半導體設備增 12.4 億美元
(+34.9%)，交通運輸設備減 1.4 億美元(-46.4%)。 
3. 消費品進口 62.1 億美元，較上年同月增 16.5 億美元(+36.0%)，其
中固態非揮發性儲存裝置增 16.4 億美元(+5.3 倍)、筆電增 0.5 億
美元(+43.8%)，藥粧用品減 1.2 億美元(-16.6%)。

四、主要出口國家/地區 
（一）與上年同月比較 
9 月對東協出口創新高，對美國、陸港為單月次高 
1. 對美國出口 274.6 億美元，較上年同月增 141.5 億美元(+1.1 倍)，
其中資通與視聽產品增 124.1 億美元(+1.3 倍)、 電子零組件增10.1
億美元(+1.3 倍)、機械增 2.2 億美元(+42.3%)。 
2. 對中國大陸與香港出口 206.2 億美元，較上年同月增 47.8 億美元
(+30.2%)，其中電子零組件增 34.9 億美元(+30.5%)、資通與視聽
產品增 5.7 億美元(+39.0%)、機械增 1.9 億美元(+43.7%)。 
3. 對東協出口 188.8 億美元，較上年同月增 91.2 億美元(+93.4%)，
其中資通與視聽產品增 49.7 億美元(+2.2 倍)、電子零組件增 32.0
億美元(+63.3%)、礦產品增 4.8 億美元(+1.4 倍)。 
4. 對歐洲出口 45.3 億美元，較上年同月增 7.7 億美元(+20.6%)，其
中資通與視聽產品增 5.7 億美元(+34.1%)、電子零組件增 2.2 億美
元(+56.6%)，基本金屬及其製品減 0.8 億美元(-20.5%)。 
5. 對日本出口 30.7 億美元，較上年同月增 3.6 億美元(+13.4%)，其
中電子零組件增 1.5 億美元(+11.0%)、基本金屬及其製品增 0.6 億
美元(+32.5%)、機械增 0.5 億美元(+29.1%)。 
（二）與上年同期比較 
累計 1 至 9 月對美國出口 2,077.8 億美元，較上年同期增 773.1 億美
元(+59.3%)，對中國大陸與香港出口 1,600.9 億美元，增 355.3 億美
元(+28.5%)， 對東協出口1,339.6 億美元 ， 增463.6 億美元(+52.9%)，
對歐洲出口 421.8 億美元 ， 增138.4 億美元(+48.8%)， 對日本出口277.7
億美元，增 58.3 億美元(+26.6%)。

五、主要進口國家/地區 
（一）與上年同月比較 
9 月自中國大陸與香港進口 135.0 億美元 ， 較上年同月增50.1 億美元
(+59.1%)，自南韓進口 116.3 億美元，增52.6 億美元(+82.5%)，自東
協進口 106.0 億美元，增 52.6 億美元(+98.4%)，自日本進口 64.3 億
美元，增 17.7 億美元(+38.0%)，自歐洲進口 50.2 億美元，增 3.5 億
美元(+7.4%)，自美國進口 48.1 億美元，增 6.5 億美元(+15.5%)。 
（二）與上年同期比較 
累計 1 至 9 月自主要國家/地區進口與上年同期比較 ， 自東協增77.6%、
自南韓增 65.8%、自中國大陸與香港增 42.8%、自日本增 25.7%、自
美國增 21.4%、自歐洲增 10.8%。

六、主要國家/地區貿易概況 
（一）主要國家/地區今年 1-8 月外貿景氣普遍走升，以南韓(1-9 月)出口年
增 56.8%最為強勁，香港、新加坡亦分別年增 49.0%、39.8%；中國
大陸、美國出口分別年增 19.3%及 14.6%，德國(1-7 月)、日本各增
9.7%、8.3%。 
（二）主要國家/地區進口同步上揚，累計 1-8 月以香港進口年增 50.5%較
突出，新加坡、中國大陸分別年增 37.3%、27.0%，南韓(1-9 月)、德
國(1-7 月)分別增 19.7%、10.0%，日本、美國各增 7.8%、4.2%。

### Page 7

7
圖 5 2026 年主要國家/地區出進口年增率
七、綜合分析
（一）受惠於人工智慧(AI)、高效能運算及雲端服務等應用商機持續擴展，
加以步入傳統外銷旺季 、產品價格上漲效應，9 月出口 872.2 億美元，
年增 60.9%，連續 35 個月正成長；進口635.9 億美元，年增51.7%，
主因 AI 產業鏈國際分工與出口引申需求擴張，及國際原物料行情居
高；出、進口值及出超 236.3 億美元齊創單月新高。累計 1 至 9 月出
口年增 46.2%、進口增 41.3%。
（二）9 月主要貨品出口普遍較上年同月成長，以資通與視聽產品增 1.0 倍
最佳，包括顯示卡、伺服器、儲存媒體、 交換器及路由器等出口均
呈倍數增加 ；其次為礦產品增 86.4%，主因全球油品供應偏緊支撐價
格維持高檔；電子零組件隨 AI 應用及消費性電子新品備貨需求升溫，
增 45.3%，機械、電機產品亦各增 3 成上下。累計 1 至 9 月資通與視
聽產品、電子零組件出口併計增 59.5%，占總出口比重 79.0%。

（三）主要市場方面，9 月對美國、東協出口值分創歷年單月次高、新高，
年增率各為 1.1 倍及 93.4%，合計對總出口增加貢獻達 7 成；對中國
大陸與香港、歐洲、日本出口各年增 30.2%、20.6%、13.4%。累計 1
至 9 月對五大市場出口值皆為歷年同期最高，其中對美國、東協均
提前刷新全年紀錄 ， 分別年增59.3%、52.9%，對歐洲出口年增48.8%，
對陸港、日本各增 28.5%、26.6%。
（四）展望未來，全球貿易政策及地緣政治情勢變化等，仍增添總體 經濟
不確定性，惟隨人工智慧應用 持續深化，國際雲端服務業者 及各國
加速 AI 基礎建設投資，帶動半導體、伺服器等相關供應鏈需求擴增，
加以科技新品備貨、中國大陸及歐美年底採購旺季將屆， 均有利挹
注我國外銷動能，第 4 季出口可望延續成長態勢。

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
 隨著法國距離下屆總統大選僅剩六個月，極左翼與政府間的政治對立升溫，然而
法國政府急需推動預算案以控制財政赤字與龐大債務。在內政危機加劇與工會
預告 11月大罷工的背景下，市場對法國債務可持續性的擔憂升溫，將帶動法德
公債利差維持高檔，進而推升歐元區主權風險溢酬並拖累歐元表現。
 上週路透最新調查顯示，在 100 位受訪經濟學家中，高達 94%預期聯準會將於
10 月會議維持利率在 3.75%-4.00% 不變，惟全員一致預估 12 月將重啟升息。
儘管美國受 AI 投資熱潮帶動、經濟成長強勁，且美伊戰事推升油價突破百元
大關、加徵關稅使 PCE 通膨至少在 2028 年前難以回到 2% 目標，官員仍傾
向審慎行事。不過逾四成專家警告，聯準會恐落後通膨形勢，後續面臨至少再升
息兩次甚至擴大升息幅度的上行風險，預期美元大幅回檔的空間小，逢低承接買
盤依然強勁。
 路透最新調查顯示，受日圓疲軟與中東戰事推升能源及進口成本、加劇通膨壓力

影響，近七成受訪經濟學家預計日本央行將於 12月再度升息至 1.50%，延續 6
月與 9 月以來的較快緊縮步伐；同時，逾半數專家預估終端利率將上看 2.00%
或更高。不過，高達近四分之三受訪者警告，若高市早苗內閣提名的鴿派委員再
次投下反對票，恐壓抑日圓表現。在歐美央行年底前亦可能升息的背景下，日本
央行維持較快的緊縮節奏，有助於防止美日、歐日利差進一步擴大，避免日圓在
年底遭遇新一輪劇烈拋售，為日圓提供實質的下檔保護。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Oct/27/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週台幣匯價高點為 32.360，低點在 32.704，以 32.508作收，較前一周收盤價
貶值 1 角 7 分。 
上週台幣走貶，周初，外資延續前周大幅匯出的走勢，台幣跳空開低，跌破今
年 7 月的低點 32.528；隨著台股表現不佳，外資賣超台股並匯出，台幣續創低位，
最低來到 32.704周內低點；而後台股低檔反彈，外資由匯出轉為雙向進出，加以出
口商積極進場拋售美金，台幣由貶轉升至 32.500下方；臨近周末，市場擔憂颱風來
襲周五可能休市，增添連續假期變數，外資再度大幅賣超台股並匯出，令台幣又跌
回 32.600上方，終場接在央行調節之下收在 32.508。 
＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣走勢偏弱。近期全球科技股因 AI 類股估值偏高及融資風險疑慮
而持續修正，市場風險偏好降溫，股市面臨修正，外資連續賣超台股並匯出，台幣
走勢持續面臨壓力。另一方面，美伊戰事和談進展的消息反覆，令國際油價節節攀
升，突破每桶 110美元上方，引發通膨升溫的擔憂，支撐利率維持在高檔的預期，
美元指數也在 101.5 上方高位震盪，將不利台幣的表現。盤勢上來看，外資隨著股
市回檔修正表現偏向賣超台股並匯出，台幣偏弱，不過本周適逢月底，預計匯價逢
高仍持續有出口商拋售美金的需求，將限制台幣走貶的幅度。綜合以上，預估本週
台幣走勢偏弱。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.300-32.800

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Oct/27/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣走升。周初中國公布9月工業生產物價指數 5.5%，表現優於預期的
5.4%(前值 5.2%)，且第三季 GDP表現 4.5%，也優於預期的 4.4%(前值 4.3%)，基本
面穩健支撐人民幣升破 6.6800 整數關口；不過周間美元指數持續於 101.5 上方高位
震盪，加以美伊戰事未見緩解，國際油價持續飆升，突破每桶 110 美元上方，人民
幣於 6.6800下方高檔震盪；臨近周末，人行中間價格連續走升，升破6.7200整數關
口，週五最高來到 6.7180，顯示中國人行允許人民幣溫和升值的走勢，終場境外人
民幣升破 6.6700整數關口 ， 收在6.6690， 週線升值160點 ， 境內人民幣則收在6.6704，
週線升值 166 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣偏升。就中國國內市場來看，人行中間價持續偏升，將持續帶
動人民幣走升，加以出口商實質拋匯需求將支撐人民幣表現；就國際市場來看，最
新利率期貨顯示 ， 本周聯準會利率決議有9成的機率維持利率在 3.75%~4.00%不變 ，
不過年底前仍有一碼的升息空間，加以地緣政治風險未見緩解，能源價格高檔震盪
推升通膨與利率預期，美元指數維持在 101.5 上方高檔震盪，將限制人民幣升值的
速度跟幅度；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH日線圖中，各天期均線向下發散，匯價
維持在五日均線之下交投，均線呈現空頭排列，顯示空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預
估本週人民幣偏升。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6300-6.6900

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Oct/27/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 USD/JPY 震盪下跌。週初，USD/JPY 大致在 158 的整數關卡上下波動；
週間，AI相關概念股遭拋售，加之中東局勢緊張推升避險需求，10年期美債殖利率
跌破 5%，避險資金亦流入日圓，帶動 USD/JPY 下行最低來至 156.67；臨近週末，
市場避險需求持續加之日本 9 月通膨數據高於日本央行 2%目標，USD/JPY 一度下
跌至 156.06，後逢低買盤進場跌幅有所收斂，終場收在 156.86，週線下跌 113點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY區間震盪，主要原因如下： 一、美國10 月 S&P製造業 PMI
為 56.0高於 9 月的 55.9且為 53個月高點，服務業則為 57.9低於 9月的 58.8，綜合
PMI為 57.6亦低於 9 月的 58.4，顯示美國商業活動雖略有放緩但仍處於擴張，經濟
基本面依然穩健將持續支撐美元；此外，中東局勢惡化使能源供應風險上升，布蘭
特原油價格突破 116美元，為六個月以來高點，也將有利USD/JPY上行。二、日本
9 月全國整體消費者物價指數（CPI）年增 2.1%，且 9 月核心 CPI年增 2.1%，為九
個月以來首次超過日本央行 2%的目標，強化市場對日本央行短期升息的預期，預計
為日圓帶來支撐。三、就技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY匯價處於各天期均線之
下且接連跌破 158 及 157 關卡，後在 156 整數關卡暫時獲得支撐，顯示逢低仍有買
盤進場，將限縮下行幅度。綜合以上，預估USD/JPY本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較
高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：155.00-158.50

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
         Oct/27/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 EUR/USD震盪下跌。週初，EUR/USD在 1.1200整數關卡窄幅波動；週間
受到油價及美債殖利率回落，外加法國社會局勢略見緩解影響，帶動匯價反彈至
1.1260遇到賣壓後回落，後續大致在1.1190至 1.1250間波動；臨近週末，歐元區10
月份 PMI表現低迷，且市場對於法國政局和財政赤字擔憂持續，拖累 EUR/USD轉
若，匯價一度下行來至 1.1183，終場收在 1.1202，週線下跌 37點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD震盪偏空，主要原因如下：一、上週公佈歐元區10月 PMI
顯示德、法表現分化，法國製造業及服務業均表現疲弱低於50榮枯線，拖累歐元區
10 月綜合 PMI 由 9 月 53.1 下滑至 51.9，為四個月以來新低，經濟基本面承壓將不
利 EUR/USD上行；此外，法國社會及政經局勢不穩導致法債殖利率突破5%，市場
對其財政及政治危機擔憂持續，也將不利歐元表現。二、美國上週初請失業金人數
降至 19.4萬人，為三個月以來新低，顯示勞動力市場依然穩健，且10月 S&P製造
業 PMI為 56.0續創 53個月新高，服務業及綜合PMI維持穩健，顯示美國經濟仍具
韌性，預期將限縮 EUR/USD上行空間。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD
各天期均線維持空頭排列，且匯價持續承壓於月線之下，顯示短線空方趨勢仍未改
變，在能有效突破上方月線前仍以震盪偏空看待。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD 本週
呈現震盪偏空的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1120-1.1270

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
新台幣利率走勢分析

115.10.19-115.10.22  
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.825%  
30天期短票利率   1.67% ~ 1.68%  
  公債－10年期- A15109 2.000%~2.002% 
      － 5 年期- A15108     1.88%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，儘管市場預期強烈颱風「薔琵」來襲，週五金融市場可能
因颱風假而暫停交易，銀行間多預留帳上資金以應不時之需，但市況仍屬充
裕，即便有新增調度需求，也很容易滿足，令短率朝區間低位移動。 
週四隔夜拆款利率區間在 0.805%~0.825%， 30 天期短票主要成交在
1.65%~1.68%，利率低位較上週下跌約 1 個基本點。

二、 本週市場交投冷清，10 年期指標券 A15109 週四於等殖系統成交在 2.002%，
5 年期指標券 A15108則未成交，最近成交利率為 1.88%。 
週二 10 年期公債標售得標利率 2.001%，落於市場預期區間下緣，並創 2008
年以來新高，顯示市場持續反映經濟成長與通膨預期。 
考量通膨前景及國際債市走勢仍具不確定性，市場觀望氣氛濃厚；惟第四季公
債供給相對有限，可望抑制殖利率大幅彈升。預期短線台債殖利率維持區間整
理，5 年券及 10 年券交易區間分別估於 1.82%~1.84%及 1.98%~2.01%。

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
 澳洲 8 月就業人數勁增 3.95 萬人大幅優於預期，但勞動參與率上升至 67.1%，
推升失業率至 4.6%，呈現勞動力擴增但市場邊際降溫跡象。澳洲央行總裁布拉
克日前指出，失業率回升至5%有助於抑制頑固通膨。考量物價長期高於目標中
點，市場預期澳洲央行本週二將重啟緊縮，升息 1 碼至 4.60%，創 2011年底以
來新高。
 瑞士央行（SNB）上週四宣布連續第五次將關鍵政策利率維持在0%不變，符合
市場預期。儘管受油價推升使 8 月通膨微升至 0.8%，但仍處於目標區間，央行
預期中期通膨壓力溫和，現行極度寬鬆立場依然適當。決議公布後瑞郎兌美元跌
至 1.2077。相較全球主要央行相繼緊縮，瑞士央行維持超低利率，策略轉為視情
況調節匯市以穩定瑞郎。
 標普全球公布歐元區 9 月綜合 PMI 初值升至 53.1，創三年新高，製造與服務業
同步擴張，新訂單增速達四年多最快，印證經濟無懼美伊衝突引發的能源成本飆

升，展現超預期韌性。受能源價格傳導加速影響，通膨壓力再度升溫，儘管尚未
浮現薪資二輪效應，但強勁景氣數據促使市場大幅提高歐洲央行提前於10月例
會再度升息的押注。
 美國 9 月綜合 PMI 初值勁升至 58.4，創 62 個月新高；製造業與服務業 PMI 亦
逆勢走強至 57與 58.7，凸顯企業擴張強勁，引發市場對通膨持續性的擔憂。市
場焦點轉向 9 月 30日公布的美國 8 月 PCE、個人消費支出與 Q2 GDP終值，同
日日本亦將發布短觀調查與央行意見摘要。若終端消費延續強勁，將牽動美日央
行政策利率預期。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.655，低點在 31.866，以 31.790作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 2.8分。 
上週台幣先升後貶，周初，台股創新高，外資連續五日買超台股並匯入，推升
台幣最高來到 31.655，不過逢低吸引進口商、政府基金等買盤進場，台幣主要在
31.700 附近震盪；臨近周末，美國 9 月綜合 PMI 由 8 月的 56 走升至 58.4，創下逾
5 年來的新高，加上美伊戰事持續，皆推升市場對後續升息的預期，美元指數升至
101 上方，台幣跳空開低，回貶至 31.800 上方，最低來到 31.866，不過隨後出口商
賣壓出籠，台幣跌勢放緩，最終在央行調節之下收在 31.790。 
＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國經濟表現仍具韌性，9月綜合
PMI 由 8 月的 56 上升至 58.4，企業活動加速至逾 5 年來最強，數據公布後美國 10
年債券殖利率升破 5%，Fed Watch 顯示市場對於聯準會 10 月升息預期升高，有助
於美元指數維持偏強格局，預計將給台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，台股在創下
新高後回落，而外資也結束連續五日買超的走勢，轉為賣超，短線上資金匯入不利
台幣表現，然而，台灣8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6億美元、年增 71.4%，創歷史新高，
AI 需求帶動出口持續強勁，出口商後續拋匯需求預計將提供台幣支撐。綜合以上，
預估本週台幣區間震盪。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.600-32.000

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Sep/29/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣震盪走貶。人民幣中間價在上周連續十天走升，並升破6.7500整數
關口，最高來到 6.7468，帶動人民幣偏升，不過由於美伊戰事持續，市場對於聯準
會升息預期提升，美元指數震盪走強，加上週間公布美國9月綜合 PMI由 8 月的 56
走升至 58.4，創下逾 5 年來的新高，美元指數突破 101 整數關卡，令境外人民幣承
壓，USD/CNH回貶至 6.7200上方，終場境外人民幣收在6.7227，週線貶值283點，
境內人民幣則收在 6.7125，週線貶值 141 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣高檔震盪。就國際市場來看，美中兩國完成新一輪談判，美方
表示雙方同意將原訂 11 月 10 日到期的貿易休戰延長至明年 1 月 10 日，並持續就
關稅、貿易及供應鏈等議題進行協商，降低短期貿易摩擦升溫的風險，對人民幣形
成一定支撐。不過，美國近期經濟數據仍具韌性，加以聯準會偏鷹立場推升美元，
預計將限制人民幣升值空間；就中國國內市場來看，近期人行持續偏強設定中間價，
帶動人民幣維持偏強走勢，惟美中利差再度擴大，預計將限制人民幣升值的空間；
最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH日線圖中，匯價反彈至 20日均線之上，顯示原先下
行動能暫歇，上方壓力先看向季線 6.7350附近，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣高檔震
盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6900-6.7400

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY先上後下。週初，美國多位聯準會官員鷹派談話，帶動美元指數
持續上行，推升 USD/JPY延續前一週的走勢來到 157上方；週間，受美國 PMI 強
於預期與美債殖利率全面上行帶動，市場重新提高對聯準會後續升息路徑的押注，
帶動 USD/JPY大漲突破 159的關卡；臨近週末，日本首相高市早苗表態日圓被低估
是個問題，使市場提高對政策訊號與潛在干預的敏感度，帶動USD/JPY回吐大部分
漲幅，終場收在 157.26，週線上漲 56點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本財務大臣片山皋月上週
五罕見透露，美國總統川普在美日元首峰會上對日圓過度疲弱表達關切，首相高市
早苗亦回應日圓遭低估確屬問題。儘管近期美元指數維持在 101 上方，但在日本政
府可能干預的陰影下，預計將限縮日圓貶值的空間。二、美國9 月綜合 PMI產出指
數創逾五年新高，疊加美伊戰事推升油價突破100美元與柴油大漲，通膨壓力劇增。
美聯儲理事巴爾表態可能需再升息兩次，芝加哥聯儲古爾斯比也示警能源衝擊具持
續性，推升市場押注 10 月升息機率升至六成，預計將支撐 USD/JPY 走勢。三、就
技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY連日上漲一度站回年線但隨即失守，目前依舊承
壓於季線及年線下方，不過各天期均線趨平，且下方尚有月線支撐，短線尚無明確
的方向。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：155.30-159.30

美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，歐元延續前一週偏弱的走勢，加上聯準會官員鷹
派發言，導致歐元跌破 1.1450的重要關卡；週間，儘管油價自高檔回落，但市場解
讀為能源成本仍處高位、難以迅速回落，推升對美國實質利率與利差優勢的預期。
加上歐洲在能源依賴與成長動能較弱下，相對於美國更難承受長期高利率環境，歐
元最低來到 1.1358；臨近週末，歐元跌深反彈，終場收在1.1391，週線下跌 91點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行首席經濟學家連
恩示警，第二波能源價格飆升將推升通膨，預計至2027年中期始回落至目標水準。
德國央行月報指出，受中東衝突、低天然氣庫存及明年醫療改革影響，通膨將居高
不下；夏季雖因出口疲弱與乾旱致增長趨緩，但在製造業回暖與財政支出挹注下，
估年底經濟有望重啟復甦，預計將提供歐元一定的支撐。二、美聯儲官員接連釋放
強烈鷹派信號，聖路易聯儲總裁穆薩萊姆直言當前 3.75%-4.00%利率仍偏寬鬆，呼
籲盡早收緊；芝加哥聯儲總裁古爾斯比亦警告，AI投資繁榮引發需求過熱，疊加持
續性供應衝擊，可能迫使美聯儲加速且更積極地升息。目前Fedwatch顯示預期明年
4 月前可能再升息三次，將不利歐元表現。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD
在各天期均線下方，且接連失守1.15及 1.14整數關卡，空方動能較佔優勢。綜合以
上，預估 EUR/USD本週呈現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1300-1.1450

                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.09.21-115.09.24 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.804% ~ 0.830%  
30天期短票利率   1.65% ~ 1.69%  
公債－10 年期-A15109     1.95%  
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.84%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，本周央行持續每日減發定存單，令市場資金充裕，帶動短
率靠向區間低位。 
週二央行標售 2 年期定存單 250 億元，加權平均得標利率為 1.638%，略低於
市場預期，且較上次標售結果下跌 2.5個基本點。 
週四隔夜拆款利率區間在 0.804%~0.830%， 30 天期短票主要成交在
1.65%~1.69%，利率較上週下跌 1 個基本點。 
二、 本週市場交投冷清，10 年期指標券 A15109 及 5 年期指標券 A15108 均未成
交，週四等殖系統參考報價中價分別為 1.95%、1.84%。 
台股排擠效應、國際債市走勢偏空，加上油價續處高檔，通膨潛在威脅仍存，
12 月升息可能性未能完全排除，市場氣氛續偏觀望，預期台債殖利率將維持
高檔區間盤整 ， 預估5年券區間為 1.84%~1.90%，10年券區間為 1.95%~2.0%。

3Q26 EPS of NT$6.58 topped our/BBG estimates by 2%/7% — 3Q26 sales 
reached NT$10.6bn +10% y/y, broadly in-line with our/BBG estimates, helped 
mostly by stronger garment sales +16% y/y thanks to persisting strong strength for 
new products/vertical orders from emerging brands. GPM expanded 1.2ppt q/q to 
31.2%, beating our/BBG forecast by 30bps and 70bps, which is attributable to price 
hikes in 2Q to reflect cost inflation, alongside a richer mix of vertical orders, and a 
lower contribution from carry-over styles. The opex ratio declined to 9.9% from 
10.8% in 2Q, helping OPM to reach 21.3%, which beat BBG's 20.1%. 
Oct-26 sales boosted by sustained vertical order strength  — Oct-26 sales rose 
11% y/y to NT$3.4bn, driven by garment +16% y/y while fabrics sales were broadly 
flat y/y. Growth continued to benefit from high-margin vertical orders from 
emerging brands. Mgmt expects 4Q26E sales to rise sequentially, supported by 
sustained demand for new products, while positive FX, product mix upgrade and 
price increases should help maintain GPM from 3Q's level. Order visibility extends 
into early 2Q27E, with mgmt remaining optimistic about 1Q27E orders.  
Implications — The margin recovery supports our view that earlier cost pressures 
are easing, while Eclat's product differentiation and growing exposure to emerging 
brands continue to drive higher value vertical orders. Order visibility extending into 
early 2Q27E and an optimistic 1Q27E outlook provide further support for earnings

Valuation
Our target price for Eclat of NT$460 is based on 17x 2H26H-1H27E EPS, 15% below its historical average in view of persisting
uncertainties on consumption trend and oil price fluctuations.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: rising tariff tensions, conservative
consumer spending, slower capacity ramp-up in Indonesia, softer order outlooks by brand customers, and a lukewarm macro

environment.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: AVC announced its 3Q26 results and hosted an investor 
conference on 10 November 2026 after market hours. We believe AVC will 
be one of the key beneficiaries from the rising penetration rate of liquid 
cooling in both ASIC and GPU projects, with an aggressive capacity 
expansion plan over 2026-28. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call (see our latest 
Flash, 3Q26 results preview: likely to beat expectations, on 2 November).

What’s the impact: 3Q26 results beat the Bloomberg consensus. 
AVC’s 3Q26 revenue of TWD59,089m (+20.3% QoQ; +51.8% YoY) was 
roughly in line with our and the Bloomberg consensus estimates. Its 3Q26 
gross margin came in at 33.9% (vs. 32.6% in 2Q26 and 26.1% in 3Q25), a 
record-high level, higher than our estimate (33.5%) and the consensus 
(33.3%) on better product mix and high server revenue contribution of 66% 
in 3Q26 vs. 66% in 2Q26 and 54% in 3Q25. Its operating profit of 
TWD16,856m (+25.1% QoQ; +108.5% YoY) was in line with our estimate 
and beat the consensus by 4.2%. Thus, its operating margin of 28.5% (vs. 
27.4% in 2Q26 and 20.8% in 3Q25), a record-high level, was also in line 
with our estimate and beat the consensus (27.9%). With an FX loss of 
TWD304m, its net profit of TWD11,564m (+20.9% QoQ; +116.6% YoY) was 
in line with our estimate and beat the market consensus estimate by 3%. Its 
3Q26 EPS finished at TWD29.32.

Key highlights from the investor conference – liquid cooling revenue 
from AI servers continue to rise. AVC is confident it can maintain its 
leading position as a thermal and mechanical solution integrator, being a 
primary design partner for ASIC and GPU projects. In 2027, AVC expects 
its revenue from ASICs projects to be higher than GPU ones. From 3Q26, 
AVC has started shipping the double-sided cold plates, which it expects to 
be adopted by all ASIC clients and some GPU clients. With years of 
experience in providing CDU parts, AVC has also started shipping complete 
CDU solutions from 3Q26. AVC aims for TWD15-20bn capex in 2027 
(mainly in Vietnam, for liquid cooling capacity expansion), up from 
TWD15bn in 2026E. We keep our 2026-28E EPS broadly unchanged.

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) call and 12M TP of 
TWD4,815, still based on a target PER of 30x vs. its past-3-year PER 
range of 5-25x [average PER of 15x]) applied to our 1-year-forward EPS. 
Downside risks: slower-than-expected progress in high-gross-margin 
orders, slow AI server adoption, and strong competition.

How we differ: Our 2028E EPS is 8% above consensus, likely on our 
more aggressive gross- and operating margins.

Company profile 
AVC is a global leading comprehensive thermal solutions provider in segments related to desktops, 
laptops, communication, servers, energy and autos. Its main product line includes thermal products 
(thermal modules and fans), chassis, system assembly and peripheral products and hinges. The 
company has been listed on Taiwan’s main board since September 2002.

### Page 5

Asia Vital Components (3017 TT): 10 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 6 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
The CEO and Chairperson of AVC are the same individual, but we see no issue with its board
quality. Besides, we see the Chairperson has 24 years of experience in this industry, which is a
positive sign from an executive perspective.
Capital management 2 AVC's dividend payout ratio was 40-52% over 2020-25, likely due to its capacity expansion plans.
Related party &
transaction 2 Revenue generated from related-party transactions was 1-3% of total revenue during 2021-25, and we found no suspicious activity.
E Supply chain management 1
AVC has introduced Hazardous Substance Process Management (HSPM) and received
QC080000 certification. In addition, AVC set up a Green Product team to promote HSPM and
formulate environmental regulation to make sure its material sourcing and efficiency are well-
performed.
E Data security 1
To prevent the interruption of information systems and leakage of sensitive information causing
losses to the company and customers, AVC has introduced an information security management
system and passed the ISO 27001 information security management system certification in
2019.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

加權指數報酬率 -20.3 -10.6 -3.5 42.3 
個股股價報酬率 -32.3 -22.1 -37.5 -22.3

營收走勢圖

研究員/吳大任 TERRY.WU@uni-psg.com

研究部調升世芯-KY 的投資建議為強力買進，目標價上修至 6850
元，基於以下五點理由：(1)3Q26 雖受到供應鏈緊張影響，營收仍
持續創高，(2)4Q26 營收將持續攀升，(3)27 年 CSP 3 代晶片貢獻
營收幅度上修，(4)新增 AI 晶片客戶，有望在 27 年下半年貢獻約
10 億美元營收，(5)CSP 4 代晶片更加確立，28 年營收更高層級的
營收，且費用控管得宜，OPM 純度持續增加。綜合以上，維持26 年
EPS 預估為 120.31 元(原估120.3 元，+65.5%YOY)， 上修27 年 EPS
預估為 198.3 元(原估 178.99 元，+64.8%YOY)，及預估 28 年 EPS
為 341.95 元(+72.5%YOY)。雖然 1H26 營運動能表現不佳，但最差
狀況確實已過，目前供應鏈因素逐步排除，且在 27 年 CSP 客戶的
訂單配比增加 ， 且已經證明世芯為北美CSP 客戶唯一後段設計服務
供應商的角色，新增客戶產品也將在 27 年貢獻，先前股價修正幅
度較大，故上修建議至強力買進，目標價上修至 6850 元(27 年
EPS*35XPER OR 28 年EPS*20XPER)。

重點摘要：

1. 3Q26 受到供應鏈影響，惟營收仍維持高檔：  
世芯-KY 3Q26 營收為 261.9 億元(原估 284.5 億元)，成長
242.8%QOQ，成長298.7%YOY，小幅不如原先預期主要係因
載板及封裝產能稀缺等負面影響，如產能有爭取夠將有望
增加約 1 億美元之營收，就與先前預估相當。毛利率下滑
至 17.95%，與原先預估 17.5%相差不大，主要是部分 NRE
有遞延認列影響。費用10.7 億元 ，稍微高於於預估，不過
整體稅後淨利仍達到 32.51 億元(原估 33.09 億元)，成長
98.5%QOQ，成長145%YOY，EPS 37.67 元(原估 39.28 元)，
差異來自於營收規模。

2. 4Q26 營收將續創新高： 
展望 4Q26，雖然在法說會上公司未提及財測數字預估，不
過 CSP 客戶 3nm 案件持續放量，先前有供應端材料不順的
影響，與供應商及客戶偕同持續排除下，推估營收有望續
創新高 。 故研究部維持4Q26 營收預估為 334.5 億元 ， 成長
27.7%QOQ，成長 607.6%YOY，毛利率與先前預估相當的
17.5%，稅後淨利預估為 40.66 億元(原估 39.1 億元)，成
長 25.1%QOQ，成長 174.2%YOY，EPS 47.11 元(原估 45.35
元)。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3. CSP 3 代晶片推估 26 年貢獻 15 億美金，27 年確定上修 
法說會雖然因法規及與客戶 NDA 的關係，皆無提到CSP 客
戶三代晶片能夠貢獻的營收幅度，不過從營收可以看出，
先前規劃的投片持續順利產出，26 年維持貢獻 15 億美元
營收不變。展望27 年，先前預估較為保守，主要係因3nm
產能稀缺，但此次法說會公司表示，在研發上持續配合客
戶 ， 而客戶依賴度提升狀況下 ，27 年的訂單配比有所提升 ，
產能也有所確保，故上修成長幅度至 100%YOY 以上，先前
僅預估 60-70%YOY 左右。

4. CSP 4 代晶片訂單更加確立，且新增另一客戶： 
另外在 4 代晶片方面，法說會當中有提到 2nm 案件將在
4Q26 TAPE OUT ，且再次宣告也只有一家後段設計服務供
應商 ， 包含IO DIE 及連結部分 ， 且公司也扮演整合晶片的
角色， 以時間點來看，將有望在4Q27 做小規模的量產， 且
僅有 ANNAPURNA 及世芯有在台積電投片該晶片的權力。 
此次在法說會有提到，公司新增另外一個大客戶，公司未
透露細節，但非GOOGLE，客戶為美國客戶，晶片就是與AI
相關(未指出是推論還是訓練)，且該晶片在 27 年下半年
會貢獻營收 ， 推估營收規模將達到10 億美元 。 而世芯與該
客戶的生意將具有延續性。 
故綜合以上，世芯對於 28 年營收規模期待將有更大的跳
升，且 OPM 將會往 HIGH TEENS 邁進。

5. 26年約略維持預估，27年預估上修，28年粗估高度成長： 
3Q26世芯財報約略符合預期，雖然有供應鏈相關問題，不
過4Q26營收仍將續創新高，故維持 26年營收預估為714.6
億元(原估737億元)，EPS 120.31元(原估120.3元)。展望
27年，CSP客戶三代晶片貢獻全年 ， 加上爭取到的投片及訂
單配比上修，另外新增客戶也將貢獻營收，故上修營收為
1529.3億元(原估1335億元)，EPS上修至198.3 元(原估
178.99元)。28年在CSP 4代晶片開始貢獻下，研究部粗估
1Q28小幅量產，2Q28開始大幅放量，推估28年營收將達到
2593億元，成長72.5%YOY，EPS預估再跳升至341.95元。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

個股報告 
環球晶(6488) 
Novara 火災延後獲利回升，2027調價及新廠效益顯現 
投資評等 目標價 
逢低買進 $810 
前次投資建議：2026.08.05 
「逢低買進」 ，目標價: $1160

報告日期：2026.11.04 研 究 員：李彝安

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 26Q3 獲利/毛利不如預期。諾瓦拉廠火災延後營運回升時程，下修 2026 年、2027 年 EPS 
至  18.7 元、 27 元，價格調升與新產能自  27Q1 起反映，目標價下修至  810 元
(30x2027(F)PER)，維持「逢低買進」評等

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/11/03 收盤價： 685.0 ➢ 環球晶 26Q3 合併營收 148.7 億元（QoQ -2.3%／
YoY +2.6%）低於研究部原先預估，主營收季減主要受
7月義大利 Novara 8 吋廠火災影響，除部分出貨遞延
外，亦造成跨廠產能調配及產品組合變動，使毛利率承
壓單季 EPS 3.13 元，低於 26Q2 之 7.90 元，除火
災影響外，26Q2 認列之世創（Siltronic）評價利益本
季未再發生。 
➢ 需求面未受火災影響。排除爬坡中之新產線，既有 12 
吋產能滿載，8 吋與 6 吋接近滿載，26Q4 需求高於
原先預期，部分客戶為取得產能願將調價提前至 
26Q4。諾瓦拉後段製程仍在重建，支援廠區本身接近
滿載，26Q4 復原量有限。研究部下修 2026 年營收至 
596.4 億元、毛利率至 20.6%，2026 年 EPS 自 21.7 
元下修至 18.7 元。 
➢ 2027 年價格調升自 27Q1 起反映，晶圓庫存較高之客
戶對調價接受度較低，整體 ASP 調升將分散於各季反
映；新廠折舊於未來仍將持續增加。研究部就調價幅度
與新廠成本吸收速度採較保守假設，2027 年營收自 
836.2 億元下修至 762 億元、毛利率自 29.5%下修至 
26.6%，2027 年 EPS 自 34.1 元下修至 27 元。 
➢ 考量 2027 年調價幅度因客戶而異、新廠折舊仍在增
加，合併毛利率回升速度慢於前次預估；目標價自 
1,160 元下修至 810 元(30x2027(F)PER)，潛在上漲
空間 18.2%，評等維持「逢低買進」。

資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析 
全球前三大矽晶圓供應商，布局高階與第三代半導體材料 
環球晶圓股份有限公司（6488）成立於 2011 年，由中美矽晶半導體事業分
割成立，總部位於新竹科學園區，為全球前三大、台灣最大矽晶圓供應商，
亦為全球最大非日系矽晶圓廠。公司產品涵蓋  3 至 12 吋拋光、磊晶與  
Silicon-on-Insulator（SOI）晶圓，應用橫跨邏輯 IC、記憶體、功率元件、
車用、工控與射頻通訊等領域，主要客戶包括整合元件製造商（ Integrated 
Device Manufacturer, IDM） 與晶圓代工廠，競爭對手為信越化學、SUMCO、
Siltronic 與 SK Siltron。 
近年公司資本支出重心由既有亞洲據點延伸至美國與歐洲，推動 Texas 12 
吋矽晶圓新廠、Missouri SOI 擴產及義大利相關專案，以支應 AI、高效能
運算（High Performance Computing, HPC）、先進記憶體、矽光與在地化
供應鏈需求。美國商務部已核定對環球晶  Texas 與 Missouri 專案提供  
4.06 億美元補助，其中  Texas 廠將建立美國本土  300mm 矽晶圓量產能
力，Missouri 則聚焦 SOI 晶圓擴產。 
除傳統矽晶圓外，公司亦布局碳化矽（Silicon Carbide, SiC）、特殊晶圓與
方形矽晶圓等高附加價值材料，對應第三代半導體與先進封裝需求。

- 3 - 
個股報告

諾瓦拉廠火災壓低 26Q3 營收，營業利益仍較 26Q2 成長 
環球晶 26Q3 營收 148.71 億元（QoQ -2.3%／YoY +2.6%） ，低於研究部原
估 161.88 億元，主要受7月義大利 Novara 廠 8吋產線火災影響。該廠除供
應 8吋晶圓外，亦為集團 SOI產品提供基板，火災造成部分製程停工，其他
廠區須調整產能支援，使整體出貨受到影響。公司表示，若未發生火災，26Q3
營收原有機會季增逾 12%，顯示營收下滑主要來自供給受限，而非客戶需求
轉弱。 
26Q3 毛利率 20.0%（QoQ -0.6ppt／YoY +1.6ppt） ，主要受新產能爬坡成本、
折舊增加及跨廠調配造成產品組合變動影響。營業利益 14.54 億元（QoQ 
+2.2%） ，營益率9.8%（QoQ +0.4ppt） ，部分受惠Siltronic股價變動使相關
酬勞提列減少 。 稅後淨利15.05 億元 （QoQ -60.2%／YoY -23.6%），EPS 3.13
元，較26Q2 的 7.90 元明顯下滑，主因Siltronic股價於 26Q2 上漲約 56%，
公司認列較高評價利益，26Q3 股價變動不大，業外貢獻因而減少。

Novara後段製程仍待修復，26Q4營收恢復季增 
Novara 8 吋廠主要製程已陸續恢復，磊晶產品亦開始出貨，惟部分後段製程
仍在重建，暫由集團其他廠區支援。由於支援廠區稼動率已高，且各廠產品
規格及客戶認證要求不同，可轉移產能有限，短期出貨仍難恢復至火災前水
準。部分關鍵設備交期較長，後段製程完全復原時程尚未確定；相關財產損
失及營業中斷保險理賠已啟動，惟金額尚未確認，公司尚未認列理賠收益。
至於 Novara 12 吋新廠位於獨立廠房，未受火災影響，目前已開始量產出貨，
擴產進度持續推進。 
26Q4 客戶需求優於原先預期，部分客戶為確保產能供應，願提前接受新價
格，惟多數訂單仍適用原有長約或2025 年議定價格，加上Novara 產能復原
有限 ， 預期26Q4 營收將優於 26Q3， 但季增幅度低於12%。 研究部預估26Q4
營收 155.78 億元（ QoQ +4.8% ／YoY +7.4% ） ，毛利率21.0%（QoQ 
+1.0ppt） ，稅後淨利17.54 億元，EPS 3.67元。由於 26Q4 實際調價幅度有
限，短期營收改善仍以既有產能出貨及 Novara 逐步復原為主。

既有產能接近滿載，2027年新長約帶動 ASP改善 
排除尚在爬坡的新產線 ， 環球晶既有12吋產能已滿載 ，8吋及 6吋接近滿載，
部分小尺寸晶圓需求亦超過現有供給。隨半導體廠陸續擴充先進製程與記憶
體產能，晶圓投片需求有望增加，惟新產能仍須經過設備安裝及客戶認證，
具備量產資格的 12吋晶圓供給短期難以快速增加。公司預期2027 年矽晶圓
供需將較 2026 年緊俏，為新一輪價格調整提供支撐。 
價格方面，能源、原物料、人力及運輸成本上升，加上新廠建置成本提高，
環球晶正與客戶協商新長約價格。由於既有長約仍須履行，且不同客戶庫存
及需求狀況不一，調價將依產品規格、供需及換約時點逐案進行，並非採取

- 4 - 
個股報告 
固定漲幅。部分庫存較高的客戶對價格調整接受度較低，公司會優先將產能
分配予願意接受新價格、簽訂長約或支付預付款的客戶。研究部預期隨2027
年新長約陸續生效、部分現貨報價改善及高階產品占比提升，整體ASP將逐
季回升。 
12吋 SOI需求超過既有擴產規劃，矽光子提供後續成長動能 
環球晶 12吋 SOI 需求受惠製程由 8吋轉向 12 吋，以及矽光子、RF-SOI及
Power SOI應用成長，客戶需求已超過密蘇里廠既有擴產規劃。公司目前優
先透過設備認證及製程去瓶頸提高現有產能，部分 RF-SOI 及矽光子產品已
進入量產。由於密蘇里廠現有廠房及基礎設施已就位，下一階段擴產主要以
增購設備為主，資本效率可望優於第一階段。 
公司正與多家客戶洽談長期供貨協議及預付款安排，規劃中的客戶承諾可支
持擬新增 SOI產能逾九成需求，惟相關合約及擴產決策仍未完全確定。後續
投資時程將取決於現有產能獲利改善程度、客戶長期承諾及政府補助條件，
研究部暫不將尚未核定的新增產能全數納入 2027 年營收預估。此外，SiC
需求逐步由電動車延伸至資料中心、電網及工業應用，12吋 SiC仍處初期爬
坡階段，預期 2027 年營收貢獻仍低，對短期獲利影響有限。

EPS 26.99元。

- 5 - 
個股報告 
本次下修 2026 年 EPS 由 21.69 元至 18.68 元，主要反映 26Q3 Novara 火
災影響大於原先預期，以及26Q4 出貨恢復與毛利率改善幅度有限；2027年
EPS 則由 34.12 元下修至 26.99 元，主要反映營收由 836 億元降至 762 億
元，毛利率由 29.5%下修至 26.6%。考量 2027 年新長約價格逐步調整、
Novara 產能復原，以及美國、義大利與日本新廠出貨增加，研究部預期本業
獲利仍將逐季回升，惟新產能折舊與價格協商進度將拖累獲利改善幅度。

從結論來看，環球晶 26Q3 受 Novara 火災影響，延後原先預期的營收與毛
利率回升時程，不過既有產線稼動率仍維持高檔，2027 年調價及海外新廠出
貨增加的成長方向未變。考量 2027 年矽晶圓價格調整幅度及新廠稼動率提
升速度仍具不確定性，研究部將評價倍數由34 倍下修至 30倍 2027F PER。
以 2027 年預估 EPS 26.99 元計算，目標價由 1,160 元調降至 810 元，潛在
上漲空間 18.2%，維持「逢低買進」評等。

## Document 250: [000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 실적은 우려를 불식시킬 것SK하이닉스(000660) 실적은 우려를 불식시킬 것실적은 우려를 불식시킬 것 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 실적은 우려를 불식시킬 것SK하이닉스(000660) 실적은 우려를 불식시킬 것실적은 우려를 불식시킬 것 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
좋은 실적이 상수인 구간 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 31.9% 증가한 104조 
6,150억원이다. 이전 전망 대비 증가한 수준이다. DRAM, NAND 관련 데이터는 
이전 전망 대비 물량은 유사했지만 가격 상승폭은 예상보다 높았다. 26 년 3분기 
영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 37.6% 증가한 83.3조원이다. 이전 전망을 상회한 
수준이다. 
실적이 끌고, 수급이 밀고 
지금까지는 높은 실적에 대한 불안보다는 먼 미래에 대한 불확실성을 앞서 주가에 
반영시키는 힘이 더 강하게 작용하다가, 주가가 1,190,000원을 찍은 이후 상승세를 
보이고 있다. 실적을 발표한 10월 28일도 10% 넘게 상승했다. 기대 이상의 실적과 
미국 반도체 시장 분위기가 좋았던 영향이다. 앞으로는 악재에 대해서 민감도가 떨
어지는 구간에 진입할 것으로 전망한다. 지나치게 하락한 주가와 밸류에이션이 버팀
목이 될 것이고, Anthropic의 IPO까지 발표되는 Big Tech 기업들의 실적은 시장의 
우려를 충분히 상쇄할 수 있을 것으로 기대한다. 추가로 진행되는 자사주 매수도 불
안한 수급을 해소할 수 있을 전망이다. 
목표주가는 괴리율을 고려해서 하향 조정 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 26년에도 AI 서버 중심으로 메모리 
시장은 성장할 것으로 기대하고, 실적 대비 주가는 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 
때문이다. 26년 급격히 하락한 주가는 업황, 펀더멘탈을 설명하기에는 과한 움직임이라 
판단한다. 목표주가는 4,000,000원에서 3,200,000원으로 하향한다. 메모리 시장의 
업황에 대한 우려가 아니라 현재 주가와의 괴리율을 고려한 수준이다. 27년 EPS 
538,187원에 PER 6배를 적용하였다.

SK하이닉스 (000660)

2 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 3분기, 모든 악재를 잠재운 실적 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 31.9% 증가한 104조 
6,150억원이다.  이전 전망 대비 증가한 수준이다. DRAM, NAND 관련 데이터는 이전 
전망 대비 물량은 유사했지만 가격 상승폭은 예상보다 높았다. 
DRAM 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 35.4% 증가했다. B/G 는 +11.5%, ASP 는 
+28.2%로 추정했다. 3분기 DRAM 가격 상승률은 경쟁사 대비 높은 수준이다. Server 
DRAM 비중이 2분기 대비 높아진 것과 고객 믹스가 개선된 영향으로 추정한다. 
HBM4 비중은 30%를 상회한 것으로 추정한다. HBM이 3분기 가격에 미친 영향은 
제한적인 수준으로 판단한다.  
NAND 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 18.2% 증가했다. B/G 는 -1%, ASP는 +26%로 
추정한다. 이전 전망치를 상회했다. SSD 비중은 2분기 75%에서 3분기에는 77%로 
상승했다.  
26년 3분기 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 37.6% 증가한 83.3조원이다. 이전 전망을 
상회한 수준이다. DRAM 영업이익은 지난 분기 대비 42.3% 증가한 65.1조원으로 
예상한다. 영업이익률은 전 분기 대비 4.0%p 상승한 83.0%로 추정한다. NAND 
영업이익은 전 분기 대비 23.1% 증가한 18.2조원으로 추정한다. 영업이익률은 전 
분기 대비 2.9%p 높아진 72.4%로 추정한다.

26년 4분기, DRAM 물량 효과 
SK하이닉스의 26년 4분기 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 29.4% 증가한 135.3조원으로 
예상한다. 가격상승은 지속될 것으로 예상하지만 26년 3분기 대비 상승폭이 낮아질 
전망이다. 물량은 3분기 대비 25% 증가할 것으로 전망한다. 신규 라인 가동 영향이다. 
4분기 영업이익은 26년 3분기 대비 34.1% 증가한 111.7조원으로 예상한다. DRAM 
90.9조원, NAND 20.7조원이다.  
DRAM 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 36.4% 증가한 107조원으로 예상한다. ASP 는 
+15%, Bit Growth는 +25.0%로 가정하였다. HBM 출하는 지난 분기 대비 증가할 
것으로 예상하고, HBM4 비중은 60%대로 높아질 전망이다. 
26년 4분기 NAND 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 11.6% 증가할 것으로 예상한다. Bit 
Growth는 +5%로 전망하고, ASP는 +12%로 예상한다. SSD 비중은 소폭 개선될 
전망이다. SSD 내에서 eSSD 비중은 90% 수준이다.

IBKS RESEARCH │5 
공정 전환 속도는 예상보다 빠르게 진행 중: 1cb nm, 321L 
SK하이닉스는 1c nm 비중을 빠르게 확대하고 있다. Wafer 투입이 제한된 상황에서 
공급 확대를 위한 최선의 방법이기 때문이다. HBM 수요는 1b nm를 중심으로 대응할 
예정이다. 이와 관련된 물량도 꾸준히 증가하지만, 출하 Bit 기준으로 1c nm 비중이 
26년 1분기 3% 수준에서 26년 3분기에는 20%, 연말에는 33%까지 높아질 전망이다. 
HBM이 아닌 DRAM 물량이 크게 증가할 전망이다. 이를 통해서 높아진 DRAM 수요 
대응할 것으로 예상한다. 26년 출하 Bit Growth는 28.5%로 상향되었다. 
NAND 생산라인의 공정 전환은 High-End로 꾸준히 변화 중이다. 321L 는 26년 
2분기에는 176L와 Cross over 되었다. 예상보다 1분기 빠른 속도다. 26년 3분기 321L 
비중은 50%이다. 26년말까지 321L를 64%까지 확대할 계획이다.

Check Point 
1. DRAM ASP의 차별화 
26년 3분기 DRAM ASP는 메이커별로 차별화가 크다. 26년 2분기 대비 상승폭은 마
이크론이 10% 후반, 삼성전자는 20% 초반으로 추정하고, SK하이닉스는 20% 후반이
다. LTA, HBM 등 다양한 변수가 있지만 SK하이닉스는 고객 비중 변화가 가장 큰 변
수라고 설명한다.  
2. HBM4 물량, 수율 문제 
26년 3분기에 가장 시끄러웠던 변수가 SK하이닉스의 HBM4 관련 품질 문제이다. 26
년 3분기 DRAM 매출과 영업이익으로는 문제가 있었다고 판단하기 어렵다. 26년 3분
기 HBM에서 HBM4 비중은 30%를 상회한 것으로 추정되고 4분기 말에는 비중이 60%
까지 높아질 것으로 전망한다.   
3. HBM 8Hi 확대가 시장 규모 감소는 아니다. 
HBM4는 12Hi가 원래 제품 사양이다. HBM 공급이 부족해지면서 GPU를 좀 더 많이 
팔기 위해서 적용되는 HBM 용량을 줄이는 방향으로 제품 설계가 변경되는 중이다. 
2027년 하반기에는 8Hi 비중이 12Hi 비중을 상회할 것으로 예상한다. 하지만 이로 
인해서 HBM 전체 물량이 감소하는 것은 아닌 것으로 전망하고 있다.  
4. 투자와 생산은 다르다 
SK하이닉스는 용인팹을 공기를 앞당겨서 완공할 계획이다. 이로 인해서 공급량이 예
상 대비 커지는 것에 대한 우려가 있지만 건물 완공과 장비 반입에는 시차가 있고, 수
요를 고려하지 않은 장비 반입은 없을 것으로 밝혔다. 
5. 주주환원. 더 남아 있다 
공시를 통해서 26년 회계연도 기준 약 100조원의 주주환원 계획을 밝혔다. 배당을 30
조원 이상, 자사주 매입/소각을 60조원이상을 추진할 계획이다. 26년 4분기 중에 배
당은 14조원을 집행하고, 자사주는 20조원을 매입할 예정이다. 자사주 20조원 중에서 
10조원은 종업원 상여금 지급용이어서 주주환원에 포함되지 않는다.  
26년 4분기 실적 발표에서 추가적인 배당과 자사주 매입에 대한 계획을 발표할 것으
로 예상한다.

ASP 움직임은 메이커별로 
차별화

IBKS RESEARCH │7 
펀더멘탈과 센티멘탈의 줄다리기 구간 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. AI 서버 중심으로 메모리 시장은 
성장할 것으로 기대하고, 실적 대비 주가는 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 때문이다. 
26년 급격히 하락한 주가는 업황, 펀더멘탈을 설명하기에는 과한 움직임이라 판단한다.  
목표주가는 4,000,000원에서 3,200,000원으로 하향한다. 메모리 시장의 업황에 대한 
우려가 아니라 현재 주가와의 괴리율을 고려한 수준이다. 2027년 EPS 538,187원에 PER 
6배를 적용하였다.  
아직 제대로 보급되지 않은 Agent AI가 확산되면 컴퓨팅 파워 필요성에 대한 인식도 
크게 바뀔 것으로 전망한다. 메타의 MUSE는 가입자당 8GB의 DRAM을 가정하고 
있다. Open AI, Google, Anthropic 에서도 Agent Platform이 공개된다면 필요한 
DRAM은 폭발적으로 증가할 것으로 전망한다. 
메모리 수요에 대한 규모가 너무 커지는 것이 걱정이지 누가 살 것인지에 대한 것은 
걱정할 문제가 아니라 판단한다. 기존 AI 모델의 진화, 효율적 모델의 등장, Agent AI 
모델의 확대 모두 메모리 수요를 기하급수적으로 확장시키는 변수이다. 
투자 주체들의 자금난이 수요를 저해하는 변수는 아닌 것으로 판단한다. 대체 
투자자들은 지속적으로 증가할 것으로 전망하기 때문이다.  
메모리 업체들의 투자 계획은 수요에 대응하기 위한 투자일 뿐 공급과잉을 만들 
수준은 아닐 것으로 판단한다.

## Document 259: [005380] 현대차(005380) 저점을 받쳐줄 실적현대차(005380) 저점을 받쳐줄 실적저점을 받쳐줄 실적 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005380] 현대차(005380) 저점을 받쳐줄 실적현대차(005380) 저점을 받쳐줄 실적저점을 받쳐줄 실적 - 기업.pdf

매수, 유지 
6개월 
목표주가 500,000 
하향  
현재주가 
(26.10.29) 
292,500 
자동차업종

◼ 3Q26 비용절감/환율 효과로 우려 대비 양호한 실적 시현 
◼ 2027년 물량–환율 영향 상쇄되며 2H27 이익체력 개선 본격화 예상 
◼ 1) Physical AI(로봇/SDV) 경쟁력, 2) 주요국 점유율 반등에 주목

투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 50만원 하향(-6%) 
현대차 TP 50만원(시총 102조원)은 12MF EPS 41,978원에 Target PER 12배
적용하여 산출. 제한적인 실적 기대 반영해 타겟 밸류 하향함에 따라 TP 조정  
우려 대비 양호한 3Q26 실적으로 주가 하방 부담 완화. 주가의 추세적 상승 
위해서는 1) Physical AI(로봇/SDV) 경쟁력, 2) 주요국 점유율 반등 확인돼야 할 
것. 기저효과에 따른 물량 개선이 주가 저점 받쳐줄 것이나, 현재 밸류(2027년 
PER 9.1배vs 글로벌 피어 6.1배)에서 추세 상승 이끌긴 어려울 것이기 때문. 
이에, 자동차 본업 외 1) 한/미국 Physical AI 투자 계획 및 2) BD 공급망 구축 
/양산 가시화에 따른 주가 반등 기대

3Q26 매출 45조(YoY -3%, 이하 YoY), OP 2.7조(+6%), OPM 5.9% 시현 
3Q26 OP 추정(2.5조)/컨센(2.4조) 상회. 파업에 따른 생산/판매 차질 영향으로 
기대 제한적이었으나, 비용 절감/환율 영향으로 우려 대비 양호한 실적 시현. 
[자동차] 매출 33조(-10%), OP 2.1조(+25%), OPM 6.5%. 도매 판매 92.1만대
(-11%). 판매 부진과 원자재 부담에도 불구, 환효과(+1.3조)/비용절감(+0.5조)
로 실적 방어. 이외 YoY 변수별 영향은 물량(-1.1조)/믹스(-0.6조)/원자재(-
0.5조). [금융] 매출 9.2조(+28%), OP 4,710억(-18%), OPM 5.1%, [기타] 매출 
2.9조(+2%), OP 712억(-72%), OPM 2.5% 기록

2027년 매출 191조(YoY +2%, 이하 YoY), OP 12.7조(+15%), OPM 6.6% 전망 
기저효과 바탕으로 연결 도매 물량 YoY +5% 증가 예상하나, 원달러 환율 -
4% 하락(가정 환율 1,380원)하며 1H27 → 2H27로가며 이익체력 개선 예상 
(단위: 십억원, %)

### Page 5

DAISHIN SECURITIES

5  
  현대차 3Q26 실적 컨퍼런스콜 QnA 
Q. 금융부문 환율 변동에 따른 4Q26 매출 및 손익 영향 
- 금융부문 환율 영향은 현대차 실적에 미치는 환율 영향과 동일한 방향으로 발생

Q. 3Q26 금융부문 수익성 악화 요인 
- 장기금리 상승에 따른 조달 및 이자비용 증가가 주요인. 자산/부채 만기 매칭에 따른 
금리 상승 영향 반영 
- EV 중심 중고차 가격지수 소폭 하락 및 차량 반환에 따른 매각손실, 금리 상승에 따
른 연체율 및 신용비용 상승 
- 차량 판매가격 인하 영향은 없으며, 3Q26 도매 판매 감소에 따른 금융 영업수익 영향 
불가피했음

Q. 향후 주주환원 정책에서 배당 및 자사주 매입 우선순위 
- 기존 밸류업 정책: 최소 DPS 1만원/TPR 35% 제시. 연간 배당 약 2.6조원 → TPR 
35% 충족 위한 잔여분 자사주 매입/소각 활용 
- 경영환경 변화 고려하여 기존 밸류업 프로그램 전반의 주주가치 제고 방안 재검토중 
- 새로운 정책 확정 및 주주 소통 전까지 기존 약속인 최소 DPS 1만원 유지할 계획 
- 시장의 배당 및 자사주 매입 간 균형 요구를 인지하고 있으며, 주주 의견 수렴 후 주
주가치 제고 계획 공유 예정

Q. 3Q26 영업이익 증감 요인 중 기타 +6,400억 발생 원인 
- 월별 컨틴전시 활동을 통한 비용 최적화 및 재료비 절감 지속 추진 
- 4Q26 원가 절감 효과는 3Q26 대비 확대 예상

Q. 3Q26 영업외손익 감소 원인 
- 3Q26 환율 급락으로 외화 매출채권 등 외화자산 관련 외화환산손실 및 외환거래손실 
발생 
- 매출채권 인식 시점과 회수 시점 간 환율 차이에 따른 외환차손 발생 
- 지분법 대상 기업들의 3Q26 실적 부진에 따른 지분법손익 감소도 일부 영향. 대부분 
외환 관련 손실에서 발생

Q. 중국 OEM 국내 진출 확대에 따른 대응 및 신차/가격 전략 
- 중국 OEM 및 테슬라의 국내 M/S 확대 대응중. 다만, 잦은 가격 조정은 중고차 잔존
가치 및 고객가치 훼손할 수 있어 지양 
- 현대캐피탈의 캡티브 금융 경쟁력 활용. 잔존가치 유예할부 등을 통해 월 납입금을 낮
춰 중국 OEM 대비 경쟁력 확보 
- 서비스 네트워크 기반으로 경쟁사 대비 경쟁력 확보 노력 
- 국내 신차: 4Q26 아반떼 HEV, 투싼, GV90, G80 HEV, 싼타페 FL(2026.12월), G90 
FL 등 출시 예정 
- 북미: 4Q26 투싼, GV80 HEV 출시 및 27년 초 GV90 출시 예상 
- 유럽: 아이오닉3 판매 개시, 4Q26 투싼/베이온 등 주요 모델 출시 예정 
- 중국: 아이오닉 V / 인도: 베이온, 아이오닉 P 등 출시 예정 
- 신차 출시 계획 연도별로 다수 예정. 가격은 원가/경쟁사/소비자 가격 수용성 등을 고
려해 결정

### Page 6

현대차(005380)

6   
Q. 3Q26 법인세비용 증가 원인 및 4Q26 전망 
- 일회성 요인보다는 법인별 손익 믹스 및 적용 세율 차이에 따른 영향 
- 4Q26 법인세 수준은 QoQ 큰 변동 없을 것으로 전망

## Document 301: AI is boosting business creations both in the US and Europe (2026-11-04)

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI is boosting business creations both in the US and Europe (2026-11-04).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

4 November 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
AI IS BOOSTING BUSINESS CREATIONS BOTH IN THE US AND EUROPE
Stéphane Colliac
The significant increase in business creations since mid-2025 in the United States, France and Germany – and, to a lesser extent, in the United
Kingdom – is continuing. Across the board, this trend is being driven by the information and communication sector and mostly by AI. It is
spreading to other business services, while developments in consumer services, industry and construction are more mixed. However, in both

the United States and France, the trend is broadening, suggesting that AI is facilitating business creation across all sectors.
Business creations by sector: a boom that is building momentum and spreading

We have calculated the year-on-year change in the number of new business start-ups. The table categorises these results as positive (red), neutral (white) or negative (blue). The darker
the colour, the larger the change. The data series are defined as follows: Germany = new entries in the companies register (start-ups) since 2008; France = business start-ups, including

micro-entrepreneurs, since 2000; United Kingdom = businesses registered for VAT/PAYE since 2017; United States = business applications (tax identification number) since 2004. Sectors
have been grouped together to ensure that the classification is standardised across all countries. For the third quarter, the German data only include the months of July and August.

### Page 2

2
The bank for
a changing
world
Chart of the Week / 4 November 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
A noticeable boom in the United States, France, and Germany, but not quite as pronounced in the United Kingdom
The four economies for which we have comparable data (the United States, France, Germany and the United Kingdom) have all
seen, to varying degrees, an increase in the number of new business creations. While the timing varies across these countries,

most of them are enjoying strong momentum. In the United States, there has been a noticeable acceleration since the second
quarter of 2025, which has continued to strengthen. Germany has followed a similar trajectory, moving from slight declines at
the start of 2024 to sharp increases. France is experiencing the same trend but with some specific features, as momentum has
picked up significantly after months of slowdown (due to the uncertainty following the dissolution of the National Assembly) and is

accelerating across most sectors in 2026. In the United Kingdom, the trend is less pronounced and the increase may not last.
Business services, and AI in particular, are driving this momentum
In most of these four countries, business creations are rising significantly in the information and communication sector, which is
directly linked to AI. In the United States, Germany and France, there has been a pronounced trend which is gaining momentum.
In the United Kingdom, the AI sector is also seeing an increase, but it is significantly less pronounced. However, this momentum is

not limited to this sector. It is spreading to many services sectors, including finance, real estate and business services. Growth has
been sustained since mid-2025 in the United States and Germany, and since the third quarter of 2025 in France.
The picture for consumer services is more mixed. In the United States, trade, accommodation and catering have experienced major
increases in creations since mid-2025. In France, these sectors are in positive territory, particularly trade, where the increase in

business creations has recently accelerated. In Germany, these same sectors fell into negative territory in 2026 following a rather
mixed year in 2025.
The situation is even more mixed for manufacturing and construction. In the United States, these sectors are also part of the
overall positive momentum in the third quarter of 2026. This shows that AI does not only benefit sectors closely linked to it
(notably the digital technology sector) but that it makes it easier to start a business across the entire economy (by simplifying

administrative procedures in particular). In France, the upturn in business creations in the construction sector suggests that this
same trend is at play, even as activity in the sector continues to contract.
Therefore, the trend is more widespread in the United States and is becoming increasingly prevalent in France. It is driven by
specific sectors of the services industry in Germany, while it is significantly more subdued in the United Kingdom.

stephane.colliac@bnpparibas.com

## Document 331: Brent at $77_bbl_ 3 calls for energy markets

Source PDF: all report pdf/Brent at $77_bbl_ 3 calls for energy markets.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Given the level of uncertainty in energy markets this year and
with little sign of a change going into next year, any call on
energy markets for 2027 is a low-conviction call. But here are
our 3 calls for energy markets for 2027
Warren Patterson
Oil ﬂows are recovering, but uncertainty remains
How to read our three calls for 2027
Source: ING
ING's base call: Brent to average
$77/bbl next year
Your privacy is important
At ING we respect your right to choose how your data is used.

You can review and change your preferences at any time in
our Cookie policy.
Essential
Performance Cookies
Targeting Cookies
Save preferences Accept all

### Page 2

Oil markets have been on a roller coaster this year given
events in the Middle East. However, we are going into year-
end with oil ﬂows from the region near pre-war levels despite
the lack of a deal between the US and Iran. While oil ﬂows are
normalising, the way oil is coming out of the region is
anything but normal, which is reﬂected in the surge in tanker
shipping rates, given the very ineﬃcient oil ﬂows.
Our base case assumes occasional ﬂare-ups between the US

and Iran, meaning a full normalisation of Persian Gulf
supplies remains some way oﬀ and the oil market is likely to
retain a degree of risk premium. However, provided there are
no signiﬁcant or prolonged disruptions, we expect the market
surplus to build over the course of 2027, leaving Brent to
average around $77/bbl. A deal between the US and Iran
would add further downside risk to this view.
Our risky call: OPEC will see more member
exits
We believe harmony within OPEC could be tested, with the

possibility of further members leaving the group. This follows the
UAE’s departure earlier this year. Post-Persian Gulf disruptions, any
breakdown or weakening of the group risks adding more supply to
the market, which would potentially leave further downside to our
base case price forecasts for Brent in 2027.
The most likely candidate to leave OPEC is Venezuela, with the US’s
growing inﬂuence over the Venezuelan oil industry following the
capture and arrest of President Maduro at the start of 2026. But we

believe that some Persian Gulf producers may also question their
membership, particularly if disagreements emerge over the
capacity numbers used by the group to set production targets. Iraq
has already made noise around production targets, and following
the exit of the UAE, some members may be looking at them
enviously, with the UAE pumping record volumes of crude since
leaving the bloc.

### Page 3

This publication has been prepared by ING solely for information purposes
irrespective of a particular user's means, ﬁnancial situation or investment

Read more
Download
Our bold call: TTF to trade sub-€40/MWh
before the end of next year
European gas prices have strengthened signiﬁcantly in 2026. But

what goes up must come down, and we believe prices could fall
sharply next year. There are clearly plenty of assumptions here,
most importantly a normalisation of Persian Gulf LNG ﬂows in
early 2027. If that happens, we believe TTF could trade below
€40/MWh by end-2027.
On the demand side, higher prices are already driving some
industrial demand destruction, while we are deep in the gas-to-coal
switching range. Furthermore, El Niño could bring milder weather to

parts of Europe and East Asia. While far from guaranteed, this could
reduce heating demand in winter. So, we potentially come out of
the 2026/27 winter with the EU gas balance not as tight as many
fear, putting downward pressure on prices.
We also have signiﬁcant US LNG export capacity coming onto the
market, which should help with storage ahead of the 2027/28
winter. If the situation in the Persian Gulf normalises, we also have
new capacity starting up in Qatar.

## Document 367: EcoNews of 9 November 2026 (2026-11-09)

Source PDF: all report pdf/EcoNews of 9 November 2026 (2026-11-09).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-11-09
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The bank
for a changing
world
6
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.41 / 9 November 2026ECONEWS
Find out more in our scenario and forecasts
ADVANCED ECONOMIES
In brief. The week was dominated by large market swings triggered by
the US midterms results. In the end, energy prices end the week much
lower, bond yields somewhat lower and stock prices down in tech-heavy
markets and slightly up elsewhere, notably in Europe. Incoming data
confirmed a picture of resilient growth overall. The Bank of England

hiked its policy rate by 25 bps and signaled it could do more. Market
pressure on France eased somewhat.
EUROZONE
Energy pushes inflation up, but underlying pressures remain rela -
tively contained. Headline inflation rose to 4.0% y/y in October, its
highest in three years, strengthening the case for another ECB rate
increase in December. Energy remained the main driver, while core in-
flation rose by 2.6%. The ECB wage tracker points to negotiated wage

growth slowing to 2.3% by September 2027. Meanwhile, retail sales fell
0.7% m/m in September and the average interest rate on new business
loans increased 13bp to 3.90%.
To watch next week: September trade balance, Q3 GDP second esti -
mate and employment (Friday).
France
EVs boost activity, market pressure eases somewhat. After already
bolstering household consumption in Q3, new car registrations rose
7% y/y in October (+4% y/y YTD), driven by another surge in electric

car sales (+81% y/y in October; +78% y/y YTD). In parallel, manufac -
turing output rebounded by 0.5% m/m in September, supported by
the automotive sector (+7.5% m/m but -5.5% y/y) and capital goods
(+2.6% m/m and +4.8% y/y). However, industrial production fell by
0.5% m/m (and 1.3% y/y), as a result of a decline in electricity produc-
tion (-4.2% m/m). The composite October PMI was revised down by
1.8 points from the flash estimate to 46.6 (-4.5 m/m), dragged down by

services. This sharp downgrade likely reflects the hit to sentiment from
the October 17 riots; however, precedents suggest activity itself should
not be visibly impacted. Appropriately indifferent to the meandering
journey of the draft budget through Parliament, the OAT-bund spread
narrowed to 155 bps (-10 bps in one week) amid a broad-based decline
in government bond yields across advanced economies; Thursday’s
long-term debt auction, the last of the year, was well-subscribed. Stri-

ving to meet its deficit target of 5.4% of GDP in 2026, the government
announced EUR3.8bn of spending cuts to offset EUR3.7bn in additional
expenditure. The High Council for Public Finance nevertheless sees the
deficit reaching 5.5%. The 2027 budget will start being discussed by
the Senate from next week, in its original version, after the national
Assembly unanimously rejected its amended version that would have
led to a 5.9% deficit.
To watch next week: Q3 unemployment rate and the Banque de France

monthly business survey (Tuesday).
Germany
Industrial activity picks up. New orders rebounded 4.4% m/m in Sep -
tember and industrial production increased 0.7%, with the auto sec -
tor delivering particularly striking gains of 10.5% in orders and 17.1%
in production. Mechanical-engineering orders also recovered (+8.3%),
while pharmaceutical production increased by 8.0%. By contrast, large
orders in aerospace, shipbuilding, rail and defence fell by 10.4%, while

machinery and equipment production declined by 6.5%. October's com-
posite PMI was revised up to 53.4, signaling continued strong expan -
sion.
Italy
Services keep the economy above water as manufacturing slips back.
The composite PMI eased to 50.8 in October, its lowest since May.
Manufacturing returned to contraction at 49.4, its weakest reading
since January, while services strengthened to 52.0 and recorded a fifth
consecutive month of growth. Both sectors reported increasing input-

cost and output-price inflation.
UNITED KINGDOM
The BoE hikes and leaves the door open to more. The Bank of England
raised Bank Rate by 25bp to 4%, its first increase since August 2023, in a
more hawkish than expected 8-1 vote, with Catherine Mann favouring
a 50bp hike. The November Monetary Policy Report sees CPI inflation
peaking around 5% y/y by mid-2027 and upgraded Q4 2026 growth to
2.2%, while cutting the 2027 and 2028 growth projections. The com -

posite PMI meanwhile improved to 52.9, its highest since February,
with new business growing at its strongest pace in nine months and
private-sector employment increasing for the first time in 25 months.
To watch next week: Q3 GDP , business investment, industrial produc-
tion (Thursday).
UNITED STATES
Post election market jitters and a hot jobs report. The Democrats’
unexpectedly sweeping victory in the midterms (the largest wave
since 1974) initially led markets to rally on (i) relief that the President

recognised the results, and (ii) hopes of a swift end to the Iran war
(oil prices and bond yields down, stock prices up). But this was soon
followed by a sharp equity sell-off on 5 November, with the S&P 500
down 4.4% and the Nasdaq down 5.0%, erasing USD2.7 Trillion of mar-
ket capitalization in one day. Why? Because of the realization that vo -
ters strongly supported AI-restrictive measures on the ballot (46 out
of 47) and candidates — for Congress and Governor seats — in favour

of curtailing data centers buildout. By the week-end, however, senti -
ment had turned around again, helped in part by a much stronger than
expected jobs report: payrolls increased by 165,000 in October against
a 70,000 consensus, while the previous two months were revised hi -
gher and unemployment edged down to 4.1%. Average hourly earnings
rose 0.4% m/m, their strongest monthly pace since November 2025.
Manufacturing activity also strengthened, with the ISM rising to 55.5,

and the non-manufacturing index easing to a still buoyant 54.5.
To watch next week: October CPI (Tuesday); October PPI (Friday).
JAPAN
Wage growth accelerates. Scheduled contractual earnings increased
3.7% y/y in September and total wage growth accelerated to 4.6%,
helped by strong bonus payments. Real wage growth remained posi -
tive at 2.2% y/y.
EMERGING ECONOMIES
Portfolio outflows. According to IIF data, non-resident portfolio invest-
ment flows recorded USD33.8bn of outflows in October, the largest

monthly outflow since March and up from USD19.3bn in September.
Equities accounted for USD31.7bn, led by USD28.3bn from emerging
Asia, as doubts over AI-investment returns and profit-taking hit North
Asian markets particularly hard. Taiwan and South Korea suffered a
further sharp market correction in the first week of November.

### Page 2

7
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.41 / 9 November 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
Find out more in our scenario and forecasts
ASIA
PMI: expanding except for the Philippines. The ASEAN PMI rose to 53.1
in October from 52.1, pointing to faster expansion. India remained ro -
bust despite a slight decline (54.7). In Taiwan and South Korea, the
manufacturing PMIs declined but remained expansionary, at 54.9 and
51.3, respectively. Indonesia’s real GDP growth accelerated to 5.5% y/y

in Q3 2026 from 5.3% in Q2 marking its fastest pace since 2013.
China
The PBOC steps in as equity correction deepens. The central bank in -
creased its use of liquidity tools designed to support equity purchases,
including the special relending facility for share buybacks and stake in-
creases and a third round of the Securities, Funds and Insurance Com-
panies Swap Facility, for which 31 institutions applied for more than
RMB100bn. The measures follow heavy stock-market losses between

June and October. The PBOC also raised the reserve-requirement ratio
on foreign-currency deposits to 6% from 4%, effective 15 November, in
an effort to slow the renminbi's appreciation.
Weak domestic inflation pressure: In October, CPI inflation slowed to
0.7% y/y from 0.9% in September. Fuel prices increased by 20.2% y/y,
food prices fell -2.9% and core inflation slowed to 0.8% y/y from 1.0%.
Producer price inflation continued to rise (reaching 5.7% vs. 5.0% in

September), principally driven by higher energy and electronics prices.
To watch next week: External-trade data (Tuesday).
EMEA
Central Europe
PMIs expansionary except in Romania. Poland’s PMI hit the 50-mark
for the first time since April 2025 thanks to export orders. PMI was
also higher in Czech Rep. (55), supported by new orders, output and
employment. Hungary’s PMI fell marginally to 53.1.
Poland and Czech Republic central banks hold and hike respectively,

as expected.
Gulf
PMIs resilient except Qatar (deteriorating further to 46.3).
Saudi Arabia: Massive budget deficit in sight. Saudi Arabia recorded a
budget deficit of USD22.5bn in Q3, narrowing by 5% y/y. But over the
first nine months of 2026, budget deficit reached USD65.1bn, up from
USD48bn in 2025, despite a rather good revenue performance (+2.8%
total; +7.8% for non-oil revenues; -1.3% for oil revenues). The 8% rise in
spending in Q1-Q3 partly due to the war will make it difficult to meet

the 2026 revised target (deficit of 4.9% of GDP).
LATIN AMERICA
PMIs remain weak across the region, with only Colombia in expansion.
Brazil’s central bank pauses rate cuts, whereas Mexico’s central bank
cuts rates whilst signalling an upcoming pause. On 4 November, Bra -
zil’s central bank held its Selic rate at 13.75%, interrupting a series of
five consecutive cuts, amid rising inflation (4.6% y/y in September, up
from 4.2%), a sharp post-election real depreciation on fiscal concerns,

and higher energy prices. The central bank also revised up its 2027
inflation forecast to 4.7% (from 3.9%) and removed forward guidance
hinting at further cuts. Mexico’s central bank unexpectedly cut its po -
licy rate by 25bp to 6.25% on 5 November. This decision came despite
above-target inflation (3.5% against a 3% target), a peso depreciation
of more than 9% against USD since September, and relatively strong Q3
GDP growth (3.2% y/y). The central bank signaled that rates would now

remain on hold for an extended period.
COMMODITIES
Geopolitical relief delivers a dramatic energy-price reversal. Mar -
kets have priced in the reduced risk of short-term conflict escalation
through a sharp decline in prices. Beyond this, however, they lack di -
rection, wavering between positive signals (the resumption of indirect
negotiations between the belligerents) and less favorable ones (in -
creased vessel damage in the strait). Brent dropped 7.6% over the week

to USD96.8/bbl as markets priced a reduced risk of near-term conflict
escalation. European gas prices fell almost in lockstep, declining 7.7%
to EUR87.6/MWh. US commercial crude inventories continued to de -
cline, although more modestly than over the preceding four weeks.
Diesel crack spread’s reaction to US elections has been much more
limited than for crude. EU gas storage ended the refilling season at 75%
of capacity (vs. 80% goal).
y/y = year-on-year

m/m = month-on-month
bp = basis point
pp = percentage point
mb = million barrels
MBtu = Million British Thermal Units

## Document 459: Token卷到触觉了！机器人终于有了通用手感

Source PDF: all report pdf/Token卷到触觉了！机器人终于有了通用手感.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

蚂蚁灵波开源触觉预训练基础模型 LingBot-Touch ，通过 " 统⼀坐标 + 分⼊⼝ + 共享主⼲ + 特
征空间预测 " 架构，将磁触觉、视触觉、压阻式触觉三类传感器信号统⼀编码为触觉
Token 。实测显⽰，接⼊后机器⼈成功率平均提升 20+ 百分点，且对从未出现在训练数据中
的新传感器同样有效。代码与权重已全部开源。
摘要：
太反差了。
明明给机器⼈装上了触觉，它咋就变得更笨了呢 ……
△图⽚由 AI ⽣成
事情是这样的。
最近有个团队做了个实验，他们在Sharpa Wave 灵巧⼿的五个⼿指上都装上了触觉传感器，任
务是让它把⼀个袋⼦的拉链拉上。
他们选⽤的动作模型是π0.5，在只靠摄像头的情况下，成功率来到了39%。
然后团队再把指尖传感器的读数原封不动地喂给它，按理来说，机器⼈多了⼀种感官，应当是
拉拉链会更得⼼应⼿⼀些。
但您猜怎么着。
AUV （哎呦喂） …… 成功率直接掉到了 15% ！
⽽且这还不是个例。
同样是 Sharpa Wave 灵巧⼿和 π0.5 这个组合，接上原始触觉后去做交接盒⼦的任务，成功率从

57% 掉到 48% 。
即便是把灵巧⼿换成 XHand ， 6 项任务平均下来，成功率也从 49.71% 降到了 48% 。
那么机器⼈多了⼀种感官，反倒变得笨⼿笨脚的原因到底是什么？
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

答案是，问题出在了读数这件事⼉上。
因为触觉数据和其它数据还不太⼀样，机器⼈的⼿指⼤部分时间是不接触物体的，所以读数差
不多就是⼀⼤⽚ “0” ；即使开始接触物体，信号也是稀疏且带噪声的那种。
加之不同的传感器还各司其职，视触觉负责拍指尖软㬵的形变，磁触觉负责测磁场，压阻式触
觉负责记电阻 …… 数据，不尽相同。
△三类触觉传感器怎么感知接触，各有什么取舍
例如对于捏住⼀个瓶盖这个任务来说，三种传感器能给出的是三种完全不⼀样的数字，动作模
型看不懂不说，还得硬⽤ …… 可以说在帮倒忙了。
那此局⼜该怎么破呢？
蚂蚁灵波给出了⼀种解法 —— 在传感器和动作模型中间加⼀个 “ 翻译 ”。
（注：上⾯的实验便是蚂蚁灵波做的。）
简单来说，就是先把各种传感器读到的数据，翻译成动作模型能看懂的那种，例如⼿指碰在哪
⼉、⽤了多⼤劲、⼒往哪边偏、有没有打滑，然后再交给它。
还是那个 π0.5 ，还是那个拉拉链的任务，如此操作下来，成功率便直接飙升到了59%，甚⾄⽐
完全不⽤触觉还⾼ 20 个百分点；交接盒⼦这个任务的成功率，也从 48% 回到了 63% 。

⽽这个 “ 翻译 ” ，便是蚂蚁灵波今天最新开源的触觉预训练基础模型LingBot-Touch。
在它的加持之下，刚才我们提到的拧瓶盖的动作，就变得⾮常丝滑了：
要知道，拧瓶盖很容易翻⻋的地⽅，恰恰在指腹和瓶盖贴在⼀起的那⼀⼩块：捏得稍微松⼀
点，指腹就会在盖⾯上打滑，⼿腕转了半圈，盖⼦却纹丝不动。⽽这⼀幕，正是摄像头看不清
的。
那么， LingBot-Touch 这个 “ 翻译 ” ，究竟是怎么做到的？
灵巧⼿需要⼀个翻译
我们先来把 LingBot-Touch ⼲的活⼉给讲清楚。

### Page 3

简单来说，它负责接收磁触觉、视触觉、压阻式触觉这三类传感器的信号，再把它们统⼀编码
成⼀种触觉 Token，从中读出接触位置、受⼒⼤⼩，以及⼒的⽅向是怎么随时间变化的。
它的位置刚好卡在中间，往下能接上不同的传感器和灵巧⼿，往上则把这些 Token 交给不同的动
作模型。
如此⼀来，不管各家传感器的信号如何五花⼋⻔，到了动作模型这⾥，都能被统⼀理解，可谓
殊途同归。
△LingBot-Touch 连接多种触觉传感器与真实灵巧操作
⽽且 LingBot-Touch 的轻量版LingBot-Touch-Tiny只有约532 万参数，⽬前技术报告、代码和模
型权重也都已经开源。
那么， LingBot-Touch ⼜是怎么练成的呢？
整体来看，蚂蚁灵波团队主要分了三步⾛。
第⼀步是统⼀坐标。
团队遇到的第⼀个难题，是不同传感器的触点数量和排布差别实在太⼤。⽐如有的灵巧⼿光是
拇指上就铺了 116 个触点，有的布料⼿套却只有⼀个 3×3 的⼩阵列。
如果按编号硬对，那肯定是对不上的。
为此，团队先把所有传感器的触点都映射到同⼀个标准化的指尖空间⾥，再给每个 Token 发⼀个

“ 地址 ” ，这个地址由时间、⼿指和三维位置共同组成。
地址会通过旋转位置编码（ RoPE ）进⼊模型。这样⼀来，模型就不再要求固定的触点数量，即
便换⼀种布局，也照样能读。
在此基础上，三类传感器各⾃有⼀个轻量的⼊⼝，负责把原始读数转成 Token ；⼊⼝后⾯，则共
⽤同⼀个触觉 Transformer 主⼲。
也就是说，传感器之间的差异交给各⾃的⼊⼝去消化，接触本⾝的规律，则放到主⼲⾥⼀起
学。

### Page 4

△ LingBot-Touch 整体架构，三类传感器分⼊⼝、共⽤⼀个主⼲
第⼆步是在特征空间⾥做预测。
坐标统⼀之后，团队⼜碰上了第⼆个坑，也就是开头我们提到的那⼀⼤⽚ “0” 。
如果让模型去还原原始读数，它很快就会找到⼀条捷径，只要把 “ 没碰到 ” 的背景学好，损失就能
降得很低。可这样⼀来，分数虽然好看，真正有⽤的接触信息却没学到多少。
所以 LingBot-Touch 选择了JEPA 路线。
具体来说，训练时模型会随机遮住90%的触点，⽽且整段时间都遮着；然后，模型要根据剩下
10% 的变化，在特征空间⾥预测被遮住部分的表征，⽆需逐点还原读数。
如此操作之下，效果可谓是⽴竿⻅影，同样⽤ 1000 ⼩时数据训练， LingBot-Touch 判断接触中⼼
的误差是 0.90 毫⽶，⽽ VAE 和 MAE 都在 3 毫⽶以上。
再⽐如，⽤⼀段触觉去找对应的视频画⾯， LingBot-Touch 的前 5 召回率是 32.07% ，另外两种⽅
法则都不到 6% 。
△同样的数据，特征空间预测与还原原始读数的效果对⽐

第三步是扩充数据，防⽌模型死记硬背。
LingBot-Touch 的预训练数据⼀共有4983 ⼩时、约13.3 亿帧。其中三分之⼆来⾃⼈戴着触觉⼿套
⼲活，剩下三分之⼀来⾃遥操作 Sharpa Wave 双臂机器⼈。
之所以⼤量⽤⼿套，是因为它不需要机器⼈，⼀个⼈⼀天就能在普通环境⾥录上好⼏个⼩时。
⽽且这些数据只标注了开、关、装配、倒⽔这类任务类别，没有⼀帧标过⼒的⼤⼩。

### Page 5

△预训练数据的构成，覆盖三类传感原理
不过，数据攒够了，团队还得防着模型 “ 偷懒 ” 。
因为预训练⾥只有三种传感器布局，模型很可能直接把这三套触点位置背下来，等碰到第四种
布局就抓瞎了。
所以在训练时，团队会随机改变触点的采样密度，再把每个触点的位置⼩幅挪⼀挪，逼着模型
去学接触本⾝的规律。
在这三步⾛完之后，就该上真机检验了。
测试覆盖了两款灵巧⼿，其中⾼⾃由度的 Sharpa Wave 做20 项任务，低⾃由度的 XHand 做 6 项，
⼀共26 项。
参与测试的动作模型有四种，分别是 Diffusion Policy 、 π0.5 、 LingBot-VLA 和 T-Rex 。每种模型
都要在不⽤触觉、直接喂原始信号、接⼊ LingBot-Touch 这三种设置下分别测试。
结果，在两款⼿上，四种模型都是接⼊ LingBot-Touch 的那⼀组平均成功率最⾼。
和不⽤触觉相⽐， T-Rex 、 π0.5 、 LingBot-VLA 的成功率分别提升了 23.18 、 21.81 、 24.81 个百

分点，⽽付出的代价，只是参数量多了 0.13% 到 0.16% 。

### Page 6

△两款灵巧⼿上，四种动作模型在三种设置下的平均成功率
就拿单⼿插吸管来说，这个任务的难点在于刺破封膜的那⼀下。吸管抵住透明封膜往下压，阻
⼒越来越⼤，膜⼀破，⼿上会突然松掉。
摄像头很难分清膜是被压凹了还是已经被扎穿，但指尖可以。要是收⼒慢上⼀步，吸管就会直
接冲到杯底。
当然，最能说明问题的，还得是 XHand 这组实验。
XHand ⽤的磁触觉传感器，型号从来没出现在预训练数据⾥；⽽且预训练中的磁触觉数据，全
部来⾃⼈戴的⼿套。接⼊ XHand 时， LingBot-Touch 更是⼀个参数都没改。
即便如此， π0.5 在 6 项任务上的平均成功率，还是从直接喂原始信号时的 48.00% 升到了
74.17% 。
其中分离叠杯这⼀项，成功率更是从 32.5% 翻了⼀倍多，达到了 72.5% 。
这个任务要求灵巧⼿只取⾛最上⾯的那⼀个杯⼦，它得⽤⼀只⼿扶住整摞杯⼦，另⼀只⼿捏着
杯沿往外抽。如果阻⼒突然变⼩、指腹还稳稳贴着杯沿，说明杯⼦已经松脱，得⻢上收⼒；如
果是指腹⾃⼰在杯沿上打滑，那就该重新抓。

这两种情况，⼿感截然不同。
换句话说，⼈⼿攒下的经验，帮机器⼈读懂了⼀只它从没摸过的⼿。
除此之外，团队还拿 LingBot-Touch 和此前的触觉基础模型 FTP-1 做了对⽐。
在 Sharpa Wave 上判断接触中⼼时， FTP-1 的误差是 2.19 毫⽶， LingBot-Touch 则是 0.12 毫⽶；
在 XHand 上，误差也从 5.89 毫⽶降到了 0.16 毫⽶。

### Page 7

要知道， FTP-1 在这两只⼿上还各⾃配了专⻔的适配器，⽽ LingBot-Touch ⽤的是同⼀个冻结的
编码器。
△与 FTP-1 在两款灵巧⼿上的对⽐
另外， LingBot-Touch 的Scaling Law也已经初步显现出来了。
Tiny 版在 4000 ⼩时左右开始饱和，换成容量更⼤的 Small 版，到 5000 ⼩时效果还在往上涨；真机
任务的成功率，同样随着预训练数据的增加⽽稳定提升。
△预训练数据量与模型规模对效果的影响
补⻬了触觉的拼图
看完技术细节，我们再把 LingBot-Touch 放回蚂蚁灵波整条技术路线⾥，就能更清楚地看到它的
位置。
在此之前，灵波的通⽤⼤脑已经铺开了三块能⼒，空间感知有 LingBot-Vision 和 LingBot-Depth
2.0 ，环境交互有 LingBot-Video 和 LingBot-World 2.0 ，灵巧操作则有 LingBot-VLA 2.0 和 LingBot-
VA 2.0 。

### Page 8

可以看到，这⼏块能⼒⼤多在解决碰到之前的事，⽐如东西在哪、⼿该伸到哪、下⼀步该⼲什
么。
不过⼀旦⼿指碰上物体，视觉能提供的信息就少了，⽐如指腹贴着瓶盖的地⽅会被⼿挡住，钥
匙插进锁孔之后更是什么都看不⻅。
LingBot-Touch 补上的，正是这⼀块。⽤⼀句话概括，就是视觉管接触之前，触觉管接触之后。
双⼿开锁就是⼀个典型的例⼦。
⼀只⼿握着挂锁，另⼀只⼿捏着钥匙对准锁孔，这⼀步主要靠眼睛；可钥匙往⾥⼀推，就有可
能把锁顶歪，推到⼀半卡住了，还得退⼀点、换个⻆度再试。
等钥匙进了锁孔，摄像头基本就帮不上忙了，灵巧⼿只能靠钥匙传回指尖的阻⼒来判断。
看到这⾥，或许有⼩伙伴要问了，灵波为什么要把触觉单独做成⼀个基础模型？
原因在于，每只⼿的控制⽅式都不⼀样，但它们都得理解接触。
如果把 “ 理解接触 ” 这件事单独拎出来，做成可以复⽤的⼀层，那么⼀款硬件攒下的经验，就能成
为下⼀款机器⼈的起点。
这个思路，其实和灵波之前做 LingBot-VLA 是⼀脉相承的。
VLA 研究的是同⼀个⼤脑怎么驱动不同构型的机器⼈，也就是 “ ⼀脑多机 ” ；到了 Touch 这⾥，问

题⼜往前推了⼀步，变成了不同的⼿、不同的传感器攒下的接触经验，能不能放在⼀起学，再
⽤到新机器⼈⾝上。
⽽前⾯ XHand 那组结果，算是给出了⼀个初步的答案。
LingBot-Touch 的⼏个设计选择，也正是从这个⽬标倒推出来的。⽤⼿套采数据，是因为⼿套不
依赖机器⼈本体，数据规模才扩得上去；⼊⼝按传感原理设计、不按产品型号设计，是为了给
以后的新传感器留出位置。
这也是灵波⼀直强调的具⾝原⽣，即从机器⼈在物理世界⾥怎么感知、怎么动⼿出发，来决定
模型应该怎么设计、⽤什么数据训练。
⽬前， LingBot-Touch 已经和 LingBot-VLA 、 LingBot-Depth 完成融合，⽤来提升通⽤⼤脑⼿眼协
同、完成精细操作的能⼒。
在落地⽅⾯，灵波也正和乐聚、钛虎等本体伙伴，以及国⼤药房、隆盛等场景伙伴，在零售分
拣、物流分拣和⼯业场景⾥开展商业化测试。
⼤模型越强，具⾝原⽣越不能缺席
聊完 LingBot-Touch 本⾝，我们再把视⻆拉远⼀点。
今年 9 ⽉ GPT-6 Astra 发布之后， RoboDojo 团队让它在零样本的条件下跑了 42 项仿真操作任务，

结果它的综合得分在 43 个参评条⽬⾥排到了第⼀。

### Page 9

说实话，这件事让具⾝圈⼀度挺慌的。
但在同⼀份报告⾥，还有另⼀组数字。 GPT-6 Astra 在精度维度的成功率只有 4% ，⽽表现最好的
公开策略是 20.42% ；插管、搭塔这类需要精确接触的任务，它⼏乎全军覆没，真机测试也因为
安全问题被叫停。
报告把这个差距归结为缺少物理常识，也就是对接触、⼒和碰撞的理解。
⽽就在今天， Anthropic ⼜发布了 Claude Fable 5.2 ，⼤模型控制机器⼈的进度条⼜往前推了⼀
步。
据 Anthropic 官⽅博客公布的内部测试结果， Fable 5.2 在 RoboDojo 仿真测试中的平均成功率达
到64.9%，是同⼀套测试框架下 GPT-6 Astra （ 29.8% ）的两倍还多，也超过了⼤多数专⻔为控
制机器⼈打造的 AI 系统。
官⽅放出的演⽰⾥，由 Fable 5.2 控制的机器⼈还能解魔⽅、拧瓶盖、画画。不过， RoboDojo 考
的，是看着摄像头画⾯去控制⼀台仿真的双臂机器⼈。
从 Fable 5.1 到 Fable 5.2 ，前后两个多⽉，同⼀套测试⾥的成功率就从 27.6% 涨到了 64.9% 。通

⽤⼤模型理解任务、推理规划，乃⾄直接上⼿操作的本事，都在⾁眼可⻅地变强。
但在我们看来，这并不意味着具⾝模型就该退到⼀边，只管 “ 动⼿ ” 。
仿真⾥的⾼分，和在真实世界⾥稳稳地把活⼲完，中间还隔着物理世界的泛化性。换⼀只⼿、
换⼀种材质、换⼀个从没⻅过的场景，机器⼈还能不能⼲好？这个问题绕不过去，得由从物理
世界⾥原⽣⻓出来的⼤脑⾃⼰去啃。
蚂蚁灵波 CEO朱兴的判断也是如此。
他认为， GPT-6 Astra 、 Claude Fable 5.2 这些数字世界⼤语⾔模型的进展，确实让机器⼈的复
杂任务推理和物理操作能⼒更强，灵波也在积极吸收；但要真正跑通操作闭环， “ 物理操作泛化
性这块硬⻣头还是绕不开 ” ，很⼤程度上仍要靠具⾝基础模型⾃⾝的突破。
在他看来，通⽤⼤模型和具⾝基础模型更接近1+1>2的关系，两者各⾃突破、相互促进，共同加
速具⾝智能产业的成熟。
LingBot-Touch ，就是灵波啃这块硬⻣头的⼀次尝试。
触觉是⼀种物理世界原⽣的模态。互联⽹上有海量的⽂字、图⽚和视频，却⼏乎没有哪个⽹⻚

记录过，拇指捏瓶盖时到底⽤了⼏⽜的⼒。这类经验，只能在真实交互⾥⼀点点攒，⽽它恰恰
是物理世界泛化性的根基之⼀。
例如灵巧⼿摸⿇将，靠的就是这种经验。
⼆筒和三筒摸上去都是圆圈，想分清楚，得摸出⼀共有⼏个圆、各⾃排在哪⼉，⽽这些线索全
藏在指腹下浅浅的刻纹⾥。
过去，触觉⼀直是零零散散的，每家传感器⼀套数据、⼀套模型，换⼀只⼿就得从头再来。⽽
LingBot-Touch 证明了，触觉也可以有统⼀的表征。

### Page 10

如果再往前推⼀步， LingBot-Touch 这套办法，即统⼀坐标、⼊⼝分开、主⼲共享、在特征空间
⾥做预测，其实没有哪⼀环是只属于触觉的。
这套思路，也贯穿在蚂蚁灵波整条具⾝原⽣路线⾥。按照规划，灵波会持续探索物理操作泛化
性的上限，打造跨构型的机器⼈⾏业通⽤⼤脑，推动视觉、深度、触觉乃⾄温度等更多物理模
态在统⼀模型中对⻬。⾄于机器⼈以后还会多出哪些感知⽅式，现在谁也说不准，但⾄少，已
经有了⼀条可以参照的路。
这也正是蚂蚁灵波想做的事情，在硬件和场景之间做⼀层智能，把不同本体、不同传感器积累
下来的物理经验，转化成可以共享的通⽤能⼒。
⽽且，蚂蚁灵波是以开源、可拆分的⽅式把这层能⼒给出去的，并且会在推进商业化的同时，
持续向社区开源开放。
LingBot-Touch 可以单独拿去⽤，接在 π0.5 、 Diffusion Policy 这些别家的动作模型上，⼀样有
效；传感器⼚商可以继续⾛⾃⼰的技术路线，整机⼚也不⽤为了某个模型去换传感器。
奥迪今年初披露过⼀个数字，全⾏业线束⽣产与装配的⾃动化⽐例，还不到 10% 。这类边接

触、边调整的活⼉，正是灵巧⼿迟迟接不住的地⽅。
再看国内，中国的灵巧⼿和触觉传感器种类很多，过去这是数据互不相通的⿇烦；可⼀旦经验
能够共享，这份多样性反倒会变成数据上的优势。
总⽽⾔之，机器⼈⼿上有了信号，也未必会⽤。
正如朱兴所说， “ 模态输⼊的质量很⼤程度上决定了动作输出的质量 ” ，没有好的输⼊，就没有好
的输出。
通⽤⼤模型跑得越快，具⾝原⽣的⼤脑就越不能掉队。让机器⼈在物理世界⾥把活⼲稳、⼲好
的那份能⼒，终究要靠具⾝原⽣的⼤脑，在真实世界⾥⼀点⼀点练出来。

### Page 11

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

## Document 463: Trade survives, but the battle for control intensifies_ 3 calls for global trade

Source PDF: all report pdf/Trade survives, but the battle for control intensifies_ 3 calls for global trade.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Global trade will remain resilient in 2027, but the focus will
shift from tariﬀs to ownership, technology and investment.
While not our base case, an escalation in an EU-China trade
war would rank among the most signiﬁcant risks to global
trade

Inga Fechner and Carsten Brzeski
Europe is undoubtedly facing a second 'China shock' – only this time around,
it's largely negative
How to read our three calls for 2027
Source: ING
ING's base call: Trade survives politics
Your privacy is important
At ING we respect your right to choose how your data is used.
You can review and change your preferences at any time in
our Cookie policy.

### Page 2

Global goods trade continues to weather geopolitical shocks.
Over the past few years, trade has proved far more resilient
than global headlines suggest. We expect that resilience to
continue into 2027. Disruptions in global shipping, shifting
tariﬀ regimes and recurring geopolitical tensions have
repeatedly altered trade routes without derailing trade itself.
Strong demand for AI-related investment and infrastructure
should continue to support trade ﬂows, particularly across

Asia, while selective US-China decoupling is likely to beneﬁt
third countries that attract production and investment. For
2027, we expect global goods trade to remain positive,
expanding about 3% year-on-year. In short, trade survives
politics in 2027 because companies can still adapt faster
than governments can restrict them.
Our risky call: Ownership matters more than
trade
Tariﬀs were only the ﬁrst step. By 2027, the real battleground shifts
to capital, technology and ownership. As companies invest behind

tariﬀ walls, governments stop merely screening new investment
and begin requiring companies to restructure, separate sensitive
operations or divest existing strategic assets.
A foreign-owned battery plant, semiconductor facility, or data
centre once supported for bringing local investment and jobs is
now treated as a national-security risk. Companies that once
navigated geopolitical tensions through diversiﬁcation ﬁnd
themselves forced to choose sides. The question is no longer who

exports the most, but who controls the productive capacity on
which economies depend. Trade would continue, but the corporate
structures that have kept it moving would start to fracture as
governments alter trade ﬂows. This could potentially cause global
goods trade growth to stall or even contract in 2027.
Our bold call: EU-China trade war

The EU and China enter an outright trade conﬂict. Europe abandons

its largely open-market approach and increasingly applies
reciprocity principles to market access and investment. Chinese
ﬁrms investing in strategic sectors face mandatory 50:50 joint
ventures, local ownership requirements and growing restrictions on
acquisitions.
At the same time, the EU imposes broad tariﬀs on Chinese imports,
with rates reaching 50% in politically sensitive sectors such as
vehicles, chemicals, machinery and electronics. China retaliates

with measures of similar scale.
The consequences are signiﬁcant. China accounts for roughly 15%
of global goods trade, while the EU accounts for around 13.5%.
Together they represent almost 30% of world goods trade. With the
EU being China's largest trading partner in goods and China being
the EU's second-largest trading partner in goods, a full-scale trade
conﬂict would rank among the most consequential economic
conﬂicts of the modern era.

Transitioning to mid-cycle 
• We now favour a more balanced stance between stocks and bonds . Near-
term event risks and high  earnings expectations prompt us to trim  equities to a 
Core holding.  The US and Asia ex -Japan (AxJ) remain our preferred regions. 
Within AxJ, we upgrade India to Overweight. 
• Higher bond yields are improving the outlook for future returns and offering 
increasingly attractive  income opportunities. We are Overweight Emerging 
Market (EM) USD bonds and favour corporate over government bonds, but limit 
duration to 3-7 years. 
• We remain  Overweight gold . Prices are likely to grind higher as near -term 
headwinds from higher bond yields are offset by EM central bank demand. 
Great expectations 
Individual risk events, high  earnings expectations and a higher cost of capital 
point to a more moderate return environment ahead.  In the very near term, 
financial markets must navigate key event risks, such as (i) the US mid-term elections, 
which have historically been associated with elevated market volatility and (ii) renewed 
geopolitical tensions, with several reports suggesting the US is considering renewed 
strikes on Iran . These  concerns have pushed the Brent oil price well above USD 
110/bbl despite significant ongoing crude oil flows through the Strait of Hormuz.  
Higher bond yields combined with high earnings expectations raise the risk of 
an expectations reset. The US Q3 earnings season is attracting significant attention 
given high earnings expectations, particularly f or the AI sector. However, higher US 
bond yields may start to bite as debt-financed capex takes on a greater share of total 
capex over the coming year. This, combined with already-high expectations, raises the 
risk of disappointment and a near-term repricing of expected earnings growth pace.  
Despite t he current c hallenges, healthy growth continues to underpin the 
economic backdrop . The US economic outlook remains well supported , and the 
absolute level of year-ahead earnings expectations is high. We do not expect Fed rate 
hikes to result in overly restrictive policy. Our caution, thus, stems from the risk of an 
expectations repricing rather than an outright economic slowdown.

A more balanced stock-bond mix 
We trim global equities to a Core holding, leaving us 
Neutral on both equities and bonds and Overweight gold. 
The concerns laid out earlier point to near-term volatility risks 
and a somewhat lower long -term growth trajectory for 
equities. Our quant model also reflects  this cautious outlook, 
having downgraded equities to a level usually associated with 
flat-to-modestly-negative returns. 
From a relative perspective, though, the higher yields on 
offer from bonds also raise the bar  by presenting an 
increasingly compelling return outlook after several 
years of very low yields. We believe this argues for portfolios 
that are increasingly balanced between equities and bonds. 
Ways to diversify equity exposure 
We continue to favour global equity market 
diversification, with a preference for US and AxJ equities. 
We remain Overweight US and A xJ equities, expecting them 
to outperform other major regions. Earnings growth remains 
the main driver of this view – while growth rates could 
decelerate somewhat, we still expect the m to remain higher 
than those of other major regions.  
Japan and Euro area equities remain attractive as 
regional diversifiers.  Our positive view on both markets’  
financial sectors supports our Core holding view on them.   
Within Asia, we are Overweight Taiwan and India.  Indian 
equities have lagged the region this year, in part due to limited 
listed AI -related exposure. However, we believe this is 
increasingly likely to become an advantage as investors seek 
greater diversification away from excessive concentration in 
the AI theme. This comes at a time when the economic and 
earnings growth outlook for Indian equities is starting to 
improve. We retain a more Neutral view on Chinese equities 
amid a still-challenging growth outlook. 
We also favour greater sector diversification . In the US, 
we favour diversifying across our Overweight sectors of 
technology, financials, materials and healthcare. We also 
favour financials in Japan and the Euro area.  
Changes to our equity opportunistic idea s also reflect a 
more defensive stance. In recent weeks, we have closed our 
global semiconductors, MSCI Taiwan, US communication 
services and global power & electrification ideas and have 
opened new ideas on US healthcare and MSCI Switzerland. 
Riding the yield train 
We believe bond markets offer an attractive opportunity 
to lock in yields. The 10-year US government bond yield is 
now in a range last witnessed in 2007. While the process of 
rising yields results in lower bond prices – as painfully 
experienced in recent months  – the yield level itself starts to 
significantly improve the outlook for future returns. 
Favour corporate over government bonds, but check 
your duration. Still-high credit quality means that we continue 
to favour taking on credit risk to earn a higher yield (EM USD 
bonds are preferred, but we also see investment -grade (IG) 
and high -yield (HY) bonds as Core holdings). However, 
limiting duration to 3 -7 years remains key to avoiding 
excessive exposure to any inflation surprises. 
Changes to our bond opportunistic ideas also reflect a 
more balanced stance.  We close our EU  bank contingent 
convertible bonds (CoCos2 – please refer page 29 ) and US 
utilities corporate hybrids ideas. We also close our 
opportunistic idea on US 10-year government bonds following 
the recent pullback in yields, locking in gains. 
Alternatives and gold to remain relevant 
Alternatives are an effective  tool for portfolio risk 
management. Hedge funds and private markets provide a 
differentiated return stream , while  rotating into alternatives  
helps reduce  equity beta exposure . We examine downside 
capture and how beta is reduced via hedge fund sub -
strategies and private markets in our asset allocation section.  
Gold remains a key Overweight. We trim our three- and 12-
month forecasts modestly, but see gold forming a strong base 
above USD 4 ,000/oz. Rising bond yields and the USD were 
recent headwinds, but we expect a softer USD and continued 
EM central bank demand to result in 12-month gains.

2 Foundation: Our tactical asset allocation

### Page 7

Global Market Outlook 7

Summary

Artificial intelligence (AI) has become one of the most powerful drivers of global market performance and, increasingly, 
one of the largest contributors to portfolio risk.  While we remain constructive on the long -term outlook for equities and 
continue to view AI as a transformational investment theme, our tactical positioning has recently shifted from an Overweight to 
a Neutral equity weight. This reflects a more balanced near -term risk -reward profile after a period of strong equity market 
performance and increasingly concentrated market leadership. 
Against this backdrop, a deeper understanding of portfolio construction is essential. Investors should consider not only 
how much they allocate to equities, bonds and alternative investments, but also the underlying factors driving portfolio risk. Using 
a Total Portfolio Approach (TPA), we assessed the factor exposures embedded within our Foundation and Foundation+ 
Balanced portfolios, with a particular focus on AI concentration risk. 
Our analysis suggests both portfolios are more diversified than a traditional 60/40 allocation  and maintain meaningful 
exposure to long -term AI growth opportunities.  The Foundation+ Balanced portfolio provides additional diversification 
through alternative investments and a broader range of return drivers, reducing reliance on public equity markets.

Key findings

Looking beyond asset classes: Understanding 
AI concentration risk 
Investors often assess diversification through the lens of asset 
allocation. A portfolio containing equities, bonds, gold and 
alternatives may appear diversified on the surface. However, 
this traditional approach alone does not always reveal where 
portfolio risks are truly concentrated. 
TPA starts from a different premise. Rather than focusing solely 
on asset classes, it seeks to identify the underlying factors that 
drive portfolio outcomes, including equity markets, interest 
rates, credit spreads, currencies, liquidity conditions and 
thematic exposures such as AI. 
This perspective is particularly relevant following our recent 
move from an Overweight to a Neutral equity allocation. While 
we remain constructive on equities over the long term, a 
growing share of market returns has been driven by a relatively 
narrow group of AI -related beneficiaries. For investors, t he 
question is, therefore, not whether to maintain exposure to AI, 
but whether their portfolios have become overly dependent on 
a single theme or underlying risk factor.

Asset Allocation insights: Assessing

### Page 8

Global Market Outlook 8

US equities exhibit some of the highest levels of AI intensity, 
reflecting the dominant role US companies play across the AI 
value chain. By contrast, fixed income, private assets and gold 
display significantly lower AI sensitivity and derive their 
returns from different economic drivers. 
The objective is not to eliminate AI exposure. We continue to 
believe AI will remain one of the defining structural growth 
themes of the coming decade. Instead, the objective is to 
ensure portfolios participate in this opportunity without 
becoming excessively dependent on it. 
Stronger diversification through different 
return drivers 
One of the most common misconceptions is that greater 
diversification inevitably leads to lower returns. Our analysis 
suggests the opposite may be true when diversification is 
achieved through genuinely different return drivers. Both 
Foundation and Foundation + portfolios exhibit attractive risk-
return characteristics relative to a traditional 60/40 allocation 
(see Fig. 9). They deliver comparable or higher return 
potential with lower volatility, smaller drawdowns and reduced 
sensitivity to global equity markets.
